Radharani said, "If the purpose of your coming here is to enjoy with Krsna in a solitary place, then what need do you have for a parama-purusa? You will not need anything of the sort once you enjoy with Krsna in a solitary place. Anyway, let us set all these things aside for now. Instead please ask Me whatever you desire to know. Tell Me what you need from Me. Because you have accepted me as your friend, that's why I have been joking with you. ”

Devangana said, "O sakhi! Who can defeat Your sense of humor? Radha! You are My friend. In fact. You are not only My friend, rather You are more dear to Me than My life. You have accepted this human form. That is true. But those ladies of heaven still offer You their respect, bowing down to You and Your unlimited good qualities. ”

By this Krsna meant to say that although Srimati Radharani performs Her pastimes in a way that She appears to be a human being, still She is in truth not some ordinary lady of the earthly plane. She is the purna-sakti-svarupini of Sri Krsna, the original avatari of all the unlimited incarnations of Godhead. It is then no wonder that the heavenly ladies offer their obeisance to Radharani.

Devangana continued, “O sakhi! I am not saying all these flowery words to flatter You. And my intention in joking with You is not to mock You. You should not feel shy. I would never lie to you. Laksmi-devi, the daughter of the Milk Ocean, and Parvati also cannot rival Your beauty and good fortune. There exists no one, even in Vaikuntha, which is situated beyond the heavenly planets in the spiritual sky, who can dare say that she possesses the depth of divine love You Yourself possess. From the mouth of Parvati at Kailasa I once heard a description of Your unparalleled qualities. It is not by mental speculation that I have arrived at knowledge of Your attributes. Having heard Parvati’s description of Your good qualities, I developed a strong desire to meet You in person. And, it is true, after seeing You all My desires have been fulfilled. And yet there is something that is burning My heart. That burning is so strong that My heart should have been reduced to ashes by now. But, because My heart is like steel, that has not happened. What can I do? My life is useless."

After hearing these heart-breaking words from Devangana, Premavati Radha said, “O my dear friend! Please, tell Me what it is that has caused You so much pain. " Radharani made this request again and again, Devangana's throat was choked. She cried and cried and could not say a thing. Srimati Radharani, with the edge of Her own sari, wiped the tears from Devangana's eyes and very softly wiped the rest of her face as well.

For Radha's sake. Krsna was trying His best to continue acting as if He was acutely suffering. In order to make His act more believable He continued to weep profusely. Then Krsna became silent and sat there for some time without saying a word until it appeared that He had brought His emotions under control. He said, “O Mugdhe! charming girl! How did You come to love this lusty boy, Krsna? There is nothing in this world that can be compared to Your pure love for Him. Your love has no upadhi, no designation to taint it. The movements of Your love are so strong that its Bow can never be obstructed. One who puts his faith in an unqualified person causes himself and his family to suffer. Even if Krsna is fully endowed with good qualities still His one defect is that He is very inconsiderate when it comes to love. Sadly, this one defect spoils all His good qualities. And the root cause of the defect is that He is loo lusty. Thai's why one should not take shelter of someone like Him.

“Just remember that day when, during the daytime. Krsna enjoyed with You so enthusiastically by exhibiting false love for You. But that night He invited You to come visit Him in a secluded kunja. And You, being a very simple-minded girl, followed His instruction (sanketa) and went to the kunja for a tryst (abhisara). But what did He do? That duplicitous (kapali) Krsna left You there, all alone and helpless, while he went off to enjoy with other ladies. On that dark night You were left alone in the forest, where you cried and cried. Your friends were so pained to hear You crying. Even the creepers, trees, animals, and birds of Vrndavana were pained to see Your situation. I too was there at that lime, watching everything from a hidden place. The sounds of Krsna's flute drew me to Vrndavana from the heavenly planets, and so I was slaying in Vamsivata on the bank of the Yamuna, incognito. When I saw You there alone I was intensely suffering to see Your pitiable situation. "

Then Devangana told Radharani about what had taken place during the sarat rasa dance: "On that autumn night Krsna left all the other gopis and snuck away with only You. Thus He showed more love for You than for any other gopi. But after spending only a short time with You. when You were very tired due to the labor of Your incredible pastimes. He left You alone there in the dense forest and disappeared. At that lime You were crying very loudly, fainting again and again, and behaving in a very unnatural way. as you were quite perturbed. Oh! What a great sorrow! I will not forget the events of that night for many lifetimes. The suffering You endured due to separation from Krsna will remain imprinted on My heart for a long time.

"O Yasasvini! famous girl! I am one of the goddesses who inhabits the heavenly planets. There is no suffering for me. But alas! All of a sudden, by the desire of Daiva, I developed a desire to see You at some inauspicious moment, That desire has brought me here, and I have been pierced through the heart. sakhi! How can that arrow piercing my heart be removed? I do not see how to remove it. "

Lord Krsna, disguised as Devangana, was blaspheming Himself. The purpose of this was to lest how much Radharani loved Krsna, if, even after hearing such severe criticism. Her love for Krsna remained intact, then that love would surely become famous as the most elevated and intense love. Krsna reproached Himself in this way so that He could hear sweet, love-filled words coming from the core of Srimati Radharani's heart.

Krsna disguised as a heavenly lady, told Radharani, sakhi! I have become so attached to You that when I see You suffering like this I am stuck in a quandary; I cannot go back to heaven, and at the same time, because I cannot tolerate Your suffering, I also cannot stay here. I am losing my patience. It is for all these reasons I came to meet with You today and reveal my mind. I am very afraid of Krsna. Has He no shame nor fear of breaking society's rules? I do not think He has at any time followed the path of mercy; in fact. He is merciless, and His heart is as hard as stone. Just see, when He was just a small baby He committed the sin of killing a woman by killing Putana. During His childhood He killed Vrtrasura, a bull. And in His youth He committed an even greater sin by killing a calf. Thus Krsna has been a miscreant from the lime He was a new born baby. How could I enumerate the great stock of irreligious activities He has committed?”

Having heard all these criticisms of Krsna from the mouth of Devangana, Radharani said, “O most fortunate lady! Sri Krsna, the master of My life, has such an undeniable powder of attraction that even if He has caused Me so much pain, still My mind cannot stray from thoughts of Him, even for a moment. Many times I have decided to stop loving Him. But as soon as I see Him again I forget everything. I can see that You also have the power to attract like My Krsna has. Otherwise, how could I feel so much affection for you even while You are blaspheming My dearmost Krsna? You have won My heart and somehow made Me subservient to you. It is not at all appropriate on My part to feel love for someone who is blaspheming My dear Krsna. It is not possible to tolerate such harsh words against Krsna unless some mysterious energy is at work, nirupadhi (causeless) prema can be the only cause. Once this son of love arises, it cannot be destroyed so easily. This love is so intense that even after hearing so many terrible accusations still this love cannot be affected.”

‘' Sundari, ’' Radharani continued. “If you are truly My friend, then you should Slay with Me here in Vrajabhumi instead of returning to heaven. Then I can show you everything there is to know about love for Krsna by opening the Prema-samputa, the box of divine love. I cannot explain to you in words what kind of love I have for Sri Krsna. It can be understood clearly only when practically seen. And how can you see it unless we live here together? How can you understand it unless you experience it for yourself? Only then will you understand My love for Sri Krsna and why I cannot give Him up even after being so frequently reproached by the elders of Vraja.”

Devangana replied, “Even after talking with Me for so long. You have not developed faith in Me. Radha! You address Me as Your sakhi. Your friend. But this is not correct. You should accept Me as Your maidservant, and being pleased with Me You should rule over Me as My master. Whether You are merciful or merciless towards Me, I am making a promise (sapatha) to You, in the name of Lord Visnu; Radha! You are only My only shelter. It is simply a fact that I do not have anyone else except You. Without madanakhya-mahabhava it is not possible to fulfill the desire of Sringara Rasa raja Sri Krsna, who desires unlimited enjoyment."

To this Radharani replied, "O sakhi! If you want to hear about My loving affairs with Sri Krsna, then please listen. If some ignorant person without proper knowledge of prema says things or gives opinions about it - such as, this is the result of prema, prema is of this kind, this is real prema, this is not real prema, and so on - such a person, even if he has read the Vedic scriptures, still knows nothing about prema. Prema is very special topic that cannot be explained in words. It docs not wail for anyone, nor does it depend on anyone. It manifests itself automatically, without wailing for anything. But prema docs not arise in one's heart if in that heart there remains any desire other than the desire to please the beloved. By definition, prema is to keep oneself always engaged in thinking about what to do for the pleasure of the beloved.

“Prema cannot be understood by intellectual analysis. It can be understood only by realization. By analysis one can become very well-versed in the scriptures. but he cannot become knowledgeable about prema. This topic of prema cannot be sufficiently explained to anyone. It is a subject that requires personal realization. It cannot be compared to anything else. Unless prema manifests itself within the heart, it cannot be understood by hearing about it from an outside source. Krsna-prema is the desire to please Krsna, to the exclusion of all other desires. What will make Krsna happy? To always think of this in one’s mind, to do this with one's body, and to speak about this with one's words is known as krsna-prema.

“Devotees develop a special mood for pleasing Krsna. Its only purpose is to please Krsna, That is known as anuraga. It means that with every action one tries to please Krsna. When one acts with love and devotion for the pleasure of Krsna that is known as raga. Among all living beings, the gopis' love for Krsna is the greatest. And there is no one except Krsna in these fourteen planetary systems who is eligible to accept their love. In Vraja-mandala some gopis relish prema in different varieties according to their different moods. Unless and until one becomes fully independent from all the varieties of false ego that come from having taken birth in a great family, being very learned or very rich, and so on, is one eligible to achieve prema.

“People in general people can never have prema, because they are always controlled by kala, karma, and maya. Thai’s why they can never be free. The living entities cannot achieve prema-bhakti until they are completely free from all of these influences. No human being can be a lover of God, because human beings are always controlled by kala, karma, maya. and the demands of the senses, and are thus always absorbed in thinking of material sense objects. That's why such a person can never love God. And this is true of all grossly materialistic human beings. What to speak of materialistic humanity, even the guna-avataras of Sri Krsna - Brahma. Mahesvara. and Visnu, all the way up to Vaikunthapati Narayana - also cannot love God, even for a second. Even if they are fully independent, still due to the responsibility of delivering the saintly, establishing the óuga-dharma, relieving the burden of the earth by killing the demons, and creating, maintaining, and destroying the universes, they are too busy for divine love. Besides, all of them think themselves to be God, and because of this false egoistic conception they cannot give up their mood of aisvarya, even for a moment. Thai's why they can never develop love.

“Not only these guna-avataras, even the expansions of Gopesa Krsna - Purnatara Mathuranatha and Purna Dvarakanatha, who are known as Vasudeva Krsna - also cannot love. But Purnatama Vrajendranandana Krsna is fully free from any kind of anxiety. He is neither a king nor a prince. When He loves someone, due to His independence and His freedom from anxiety. He forgets that He is God. That is why Nandanandana Sri Krsna is the only one eligible to be a real lover. If one has other desires - meaning even the smallest desire for his own pleasure, not exclusively desirous of Krsna’s pleasure - then he will not be able to love Krsna.

“No one except the gopis of Vraja has this exclusive desire to please Krsna, this pure love. Even Krsna’s queens in Dvaraka do not have this pure love for Krsna. They have other desires in addition to the desire to please Krsna, This has been proven by Krsna Himself. When as a part of His transcendental pastimes in Dvaraka Krsna manifested a fever and asked Narada Muni to cure that fever by bringing dust from the feet of His pure devotees to put on His head, not one of the 16. 108 queens of Dvaraka would give the dust from their feet, because they feared going to hell. The Vraja-gopis however proved their pure love for Krsna by giving the dust from their feet without caring at all for what they may suffer as a result. This proves their pure love for Krsna."

Radharani continued speaking to Devangana, “In Vraja everyone has the impression that among the gopis Krsna is attached to Me most of all. This is true. Because Sri Krsna has accepted My love as the greatest. He considers the love of all other gopis inferior. This kind of unalloyed and causeless love cannot be found anywhere except Vraja. If we look at things objectively, we can sec that the queens of Dvaraka, like Rukmini and Satyabhama, sometimes desire things for their own enjoyment. Only the pure and causeless love of the Vraja-gopis reaches the platform of mahabhava."

Radharani said, “O sakhi! Krsna reciprocates with His beloved gopis according to their love for Him. Krsna cannot be blamed for this: there is no fault on His pan. And if there is any deviation from this system, then Krsna becomes unhappy. I too become unhappy. This means that if some gopi shows more love for Krsna than she is supposed to show, or enjoys more with Krsna than she is supposed to enjoy, then Krsna becomes unhappy because I, Radha, am the only gopi who has the kind of love to be able to make Krsna happy just as He desires. When Krsna feels unhappy, I also feel unhappy. Once, Krsna asked Me to meet Him at a particular time and place. But. against His desire, compelled by the love of another gopi. He spent His time with that other girl. Still, He did not derive any real pleasure from it, because He was pained at heart, thinking about how I was suffering without Him. When I suffer, Krsna suffers. Thus the whole creation suffers. Krsna, despite being embraced by the other beautiful ladies of Vraja, still faints at the thought of My sadness.

‘‘Just last night Sri Krsna had gone to a kunja to meet some other lady. After spending time with her. He came back to meet Me. and He begged forgiveness for His misdeeds. I was waiting for Krsna throughout the night, thinking that He would come to Me and I would be able to serve Him nicely. When Krsna did not come I destroyed all the arrangements I had so carefully made to serve Him, and being very upset I remained there in a mood of divine loving anger. Eventually Krsna came to where I was waiting, but when I looked at His body I saw signs that He had been enjoying with another girl and I became very angry with Him. I said, “You should leave Me here. Go back to her!” Though I showed anger to Krsna, it was not real anger. I sometimes use abusive words against Him, but for His pleasure. I know very well that whomever He had been with was not able to satisfy Him. Why was He with her when He would not gel full satisfaction? This was the real cause of My anger. Thus the anger that gives pleasure to Krsna is known as premotha (arising from love).

“O sakhi! During the sarat rasa dance Krsna left all the other gopis behind, taking only Me with Him. But after some lime He left Me alone in the forest as well. Please listen to the reason for this. I will explain it to you. I became proud to see that prema-sagara Vrajendranandana Sri Krsna loves me best among all the gopis. Because of My pride I told My Prananatha Sri Krsna, “I am now unable to walk. Please carry Me on Your shoulder. ” So, in order to destroy My pride He disappeared from sight and left Me alone. After some lime, all the other gopis, who were very much aggrieved due to separation from Krsna, came to the place where I had been left alone. I did not blame Krsna for leaving Me there, but still He fell He had made a mistake and so told all the gopis, “Radhika has no faults. All faults are Mine. ”

Srimati Radharani was still not able to understand that Devangana was none other than Sri Krsna. Radha told Devangana how Krsna thinks about Her, “Krsna says. The enjoyment I feel in separation from Radha is millions of times more pleasurable than the enjoyment I feel in uniting with Radha. Whenever I am separated from Radha the pleasure of uniting with Her becomes that much more relishable. The sweetness of sringara-rasa grows more and more when there is sambhoga and vipralambha, which means a reunion after some time of separation.”

Radharani continued, “O sakhi! I will tell you more about this. Please listen. During His childhood Krsna killed many demons, like Putana, Vatsasura, Bakasura, Aristasura, Vyomasura, and Kesi. By killing these demons Krsna did not accrue any sin, because when Krsna came to this world to relieve the earth from the burden of demons like these, Ksirodakasayi Visnu, who appeared from the womb of Mother Devaki, had merged into the body of Sri Krsna in Gokula. Whenever a demon was killed it was the doing of Visnu. These were not the activities of Sri Krsna.”

Full of curiosiiv. Krsna in the form of Devangana said, “O Radha! I can sec now that all the symptoms of prema You have described exist in You only. For this reason all the faults of Your lover look like good qualities. Once again You are getting pleasure due to Your lover. You cannot tolerate His suffering, even a little bit. If for some reason one docs not want to give up the body, that is called prema. Radha! Surely Krsna is not premavan, very loving and affectionate. I can see this just by examining His behavior, Radha! You told Me that during the rasa dance when Your lover left You His inner mood was like so-and-so. But how can I believe this? You are saying this only because You must have heard it from Krsna directly. You have not heard it from His friends, but even if You had heard from His friends, still I would not believe it. Krsna and His friends never speak the truth."

Radharani objected, "I can understand the mood of My lover. Whenever a certain mood arises in Him, I know it immediately. If you can put some faith in My words, then only will I explain the reason for this.”

Devangana said, “All right. If you can give Me a plausible explanation, then why shouldn't I believe You? Definitely, I will believe. Your lover, Krsna, is an ocean of good qualities. There is no doubt about this. But You are saying that He is premavan, the greatest lover. That is merely Your opinion. When during the rasa-lila You were left alone by Your lover, why did You cry? Were You crying because You were unhappy?”

Radha replied, “O Devi! You have spoken truth. Now I will tell you the reason for that. Please listen attentively. Although I know His heart and His internal mood, at that time my feelings of separation from Him had such power over Me that I lost all My knowledge. I could not properly understand what had happened and why. ”

Devangana said, “You may indeed know Krsna's internal moods and what is there in His mind. I cannot dispute this. But My real question is whether or not He understands Your mind.”

Radharani replied, “How can you ask whether or not Krsna knows My mind? . Actually, I should not reveal such a thing to any one. But you are Devangana, so I will reveal it to you, and only you. Please listen carefully. There is no difference between My heart and the heart of My lover. I dwell always in His heart, and He dwells always in Mine. That’s why we know each other's minds. We may appear to be two. but We are one soul, never to be divided.”

“Even though We have separate bodies, still in reality We are one and the same. There is no difference between Us, because I have appeared from His left side. In reality He is the form of bliss, or ananda. And I am His hladini-sakti. His pleasure-giving potency. Just like fire cannot be separated from the heat it gives off, just like a lotus flower cannot be separated from its aroma, in quite the same way, although We appear in two separate bodies, in reality We cannot be separated. We have appeared in these two different forms - as Radha and Krsna - just to enjoy different rasas, or mellows. Without lila, pastimes, there is no question of enjoying mellows.”

In conclusion, Sri Radha said, “Today I have revealed to You the most secret jewel of loving affairs (prema) from the box (samputa) of My heart. You should always hold this precious jewel within your own heart. But keep it very secretly. Never reveal it to any one. ”

Having now heard these confidential truths about Himself and His beloved, Radharani, from the mouth of Sri Radha Herself, Krsna in the form of Devangana told Her, “O Radha! I now accept as truth whatever You have told Me about You and Your lover, Krsna. And yet My heart is very complicated, I would still like to test the truth behind Your words. Right now You are here. Krsna may be in the home of Nanda Maharaja, or perhaps He is in the forest, herding cows. You just told Me that both of You are one soul, and I believe You. But without testing Your statement My doubts will not be fully removed. You have said that whatever is there in Your heart also becomes manifest in Krsna's heart at the same time. If You can demonstrate this fact to Me now, then I will have firm faith in everything You have told Me. This is what I propose: Please meditate on Your lover and let Him know that You want Him to come here. Wherever He may be, if He understands Your desire due to the inseparable nature of Your hearts, then immediately He will come here and appear in front of You. If You are able to do that, then only will I believe it is true that You both are one and the same soul, two bodies that share one heart. If, after You call Your lover. He cannot come here due to some obstacle, like the presence of His elders or an accident or a demon or any other obstacle, then I will not hold that against You. Moreover, I know very well that You may not like to bring Him here for Your own pleasure. Nonetheless. I humbly request You to please meditate upon Him and call Him here. If He comes here by Your thinking of Him. then I will be very happy to see Him. Especially now that there is no fear of Your respectable family members. Your mother-in-law, Jatila, or Your husband.. Abhimanyu. They are not here, nor will they come. You can thus think of Krsna without fear."

For the sake of argument, Sri Radha said. "O sakhi! If according to your instruction I think of My lover but cannot bring Him here, then My lover will feel shame, and thus I will always feel sad. If I cannot bring Him here, please do not mock me.”

Having said this, Radharani closed Her eyes and sat down to meditate. In Her mind She remembered Her worshipable deity Surya-deva and prayed, "O most worshipable demigod! fulfiller of My desires! merciful one! witness of all kinds of truth and lies! My worshipable Lord Surya-deva! Both of Us, Radha and Krsna, are one soul. If this is indeed true, then let My Prananatha, Sri Krsna, appear here in front of Me now to give pleasure to My friend and prove the truth of My words. ” Having finished Her prayer, Radharani next meditated upon Sri Krsna, the Lord of Her life. While She sat silently with Her eyes closed, all Her senses under control like the greatest of yoginis, Sri Krsna changed out of His disguise as Devangana and appeared instead in His real form as Sri Krsna. While Radha continued to meditate, Krsna began to embrace and kiss His dear most beloved again and again. Feeling Krsna's affectionate embrace, Radha’s hair stood on end and tears of happiness flowed from Her eyes. In Her meditation She realized that Her lover, Sri Krsna, had come. When She opened Her eyes She saw Krsna silting there next to Her and She was immersed in an ocean of bliss. Having come back to external consciousness Radharani felt shy so She covered Her face with Her cloth.

Lalita said, “O Vilasinah! It is a great surprise that You have arrived here without knowledge of Radha’s desire. It is not possible even for air to enter this palace from outside. You have fearlessly dared to come here. That means You must be very brave. This most chaste girl. Srimati Radharani, is protected by me. After taking Her bath on the pretext of worshiping Surya-deva, She sat in meditation on You with Her eyes closed. You have touched the body of Radharani by force. Are You greater than Surya-deva? Certainly You do not understand what shame is. You have crossed over social rules and religious principles alike. However, Radha's mother-in-law. Jatila, Her husband, Abhimanyu, and Her sister-in-law, Kutila, are not in the palace right now. We are all Her dear friends who always desire Her pleasure. Now You can enjoy with Your beloved Radha without fear.”

Sri Krsna said, “I have no objection. I was playing with My friends in the courtyard outside My fathers cowshed. All of a sudden I remembered Radha. Then some devata I do not know brought Me here. "

Suddenly Radha remembered something. She asked Lalita, '‘O sakhi! Where is that devi, Devangana? She asked Me to bring Krsna here in order to prove I was speaking the truth. Now Krsna is here, but where is she? Does she believe Me now?"

Lalita said, "That devi, after seeing the reunion of the two of You, has been freed from all doubts. She is somewhere inside this house, and by Her presence She is giving pleasure to all of us. "

Sri Krsna exclaimed, “Lalita! Please introduce Me to that heavenly lady."

Lalita said, “That Devangana might have disappeared, or perhaps She has left this house. We are going to search for Her. ” Saying like this, Lalita and the other sakhis left the room and closed the door behind them, leaving Radha and Krsna inside.

## Krsna’s Marriage in Vrndavana

When Krsna left Vrndavana, He told the gopis He would come back. This promise was the only thing that kept the Vraja-vasis alive, because they were fully confident that Krsna would return. Krsna left Vrndavana because it had been repeatedly attacked by the demons Kamsa had sent to kill Krsna. So Krsna went to Mathura and killed Kamsa. Nonetheless, Krsna and His family members, the Yadavas, were still being attacked by the relatives and friends of Kamsa. To protect His only family from attack, Krsna eventually left Mathura and went to Dvaraka. Then, having finally killed all the demons - the last of which were Salva and Dantavakra - Krsna wanted to return to Vrndavana. Daruka brought Krsna to Mathura by chariot. Having reached the gates of Mathura, Krishna became very eager to continue on to Vraja. He went to Vis- raina-ghata for a little rest and then bathed in the Yamuna. He was completely satisfied. At this time Krsna decided not to use His weapons any longer, as He had completed His mission to kill all the demons.

Krsna then blew His conch, the panchajanya. As soon as Vraja-vasis heard the sweet sound, their curiosity was piqued. They all ran towards the source of the sound, thinking that Krsna had come back from Dvaraka. As they approached they saw Garuda on Krsna's chariot and realized that Krsna had indeed returned. In fact, the mere sight of Krsna's chariot was enough to leave them stunned in ecstasy. But not long thereafter Nanda Maharaja and the Vraja-vasis finally found themselves face to face with Krsna, and a festial of unique loving exchanges began.

Krsna said, "I have killed many sinful demons, like Dantavakra and Salva. They were preventing Me from entering Vraja. But now everything is auspicious - we have no more enemies. Now it is time for Me to return home to Vraja.”

Krsna ordered Daruka to bring the attractive clothes and ornaments that He had brought for the Vraja-vasis. To His friends and relatives Krsna distributed an unlimited amount of garments, ornaments, oil, and gems. And in return the Vraja-vasis gave Krsna an equally impressive array of clothes and ornaments, which Krsna wore with great delight, leaving behind in His chariot His cakra and other implements of ksatriya life. Soon Krsna asked Mother Yasoda to bring His flute, staff, and peacock feather. Having soon donned these items, Krsna looked very beautiful.

Even though Krsna was now reuniting with the Vraja-vasis after many years, still to Him they all looked the same. He saw all the Vraja-vasis as being the same age, form, and nature as they had been when He left Vraja long ago and went to Mathura to kill Kamsa, Krsna was overjoyed to be reunited with all His friends and relatives.

All the Vraja-vasis were overjoyed to sec Krsna return to Vraja. They prayed that their happiness would not end. Meanwhile, Krsna spent His time in Vraja happily singing and dancing with His friends. During this time Daruka also stayed there in Vraja along with the chariot. But Nanda and the other Vraja-vasis did not like to see the chariot still there. They feared that as long as the chariot remained there was some chance that Krsna would again leave for Dvaraka. Krsna came to understand the Waja-vasi’s fear, but still He asked them why they were not happy even though He had returned.

Krishna said, “I left Vraja and went to Mathura to kill Kamsa. I went to Dvaraka to kill the other demons and to protect My devotees. Though I had left Vrndavana, still My life was undeniably connected to you all. That’s why I have returned. It is very painful for Me to see that you are still unhappy. ”

Everyone present there said, “We will be happy only when You make Your home here in Vraja. It is very painful for us to see You living anywhere else. ”

Krsna understood that by their saying "make Your home” the Vraja-vasis were expressing their desire for Him to gel married and stay in Vraja. So Krsna ordered Daruka to go to Dvaraka and bring back to Vrndavana His brother Balarama, Balarama’s mother, Rohini, and His cousin Uddhava. He also ordered Daruka to request that Balarama also bring with Him the parrot and sarika bird. Daruka did as he was told and brought all of them to Vraja.

Soon Balarama and Krsna were once again walking together on the street of Vraja just like They had done before. They looked just like cowherd boys, as they had looked long ago. Even Uddhava now looked like a cowherd boy. Daruka was surprised to see this. Privately, he told Uddhava, “From what I can see, all the actions of the Vraja-vasis are covered by illusion. To me, everything here looks pervasive and beyond comprehension. I am disturbed to see that they are engaging Krsna in herding cows. By His maya He has made even you resemble a cowherd boy. I do not know whom to talk to about this. That's why I am privately telling you this. Please save me. "

Uddhava replied, “You are foolish. You cannot understand Vraja. Not everyone is qualified to live here. Even Brahma prays for good fortune by taking the dust of the Vraja-vasis on his heads. "

What follows is a narration of Krsna's marriage to Radharani and the other preyasi gopis.

Radharani and Her sakhis were sitting on the edge of a madhavi-kunja, a grove of madhavi creepers. Radharani sat in the center of the group, surrounded by Her sakhis. Her heart was overwhelmed with sorrow. At that time Paurnamasi and Vrnda-devi arrived there. They had been looking for Radha. As soon as Radharani saw Paurnamasi. She respectfully stood up to greet her, offering seals to Paurnamasi and Vrnda. Radharani asked Paurnamasi. “Devi! Have you brought us any good news about Sri Hari? Will He fulfill the promise that has kept us alive now for so many years? Or will He continue to ignore us? ”

Paurnamasi had come there directly after meeting Krsna. She said, "Last night Govinda lay awake the whole night, just thinking about You. Though He is surrounded by so many loving friends and relatives, still He never feels at peace. ”

Lalita was not impressed by Paurnamasis report. She said, “There is no need for Krsna to Slay awake all night. He needs only fulfill His promise and relieve my dear friend Radha from Her suffering. ”

Visakha added, “Hari is feeling pain because He knows He is never truthful. He cannot keep His word, and that is what’s giving Him pain. ”

Radharani chastised Her friends for saying such things. But all the sakhis were angry; Krsna had left Radharani, and for so many years now She had been suffering and crying from the pain of separation. And when, after many years, Krsna had finally returned. He did not recognize Sr! Radha.

Radharani picked up a letter and handed it to Paurnamasi. Radha said, “Devi! We have decided that if we do not get shelter at Krsna's lotus feet, then we will give up our lives. ”

Paurnamasi read the letter out loud, which said that if Krsna did not marry the gopis, not caring about their love for Him and being overly concerned about the result of transgressing dharma, if Nanda Maharaja fell ashamed about the prospect of His son marrying the Vraja-gopis, and if the Vraja-vasis too condemned Krsna’s marriage to Vraja’s cowherd girls, then those unfortunate young damsels vowed to commit suicide. “Without Krsna's shelter, we cannot live any longer, ” the letter read. Hearing this, Vrnda started to cry and protest, saying, “No! No! ”

In one voice, Radharani and all Her sakhis declared, “This is our vow, and we must fulfill it! ”

Paurnamasi wanted to test the gopis’ determination to marry Krsna. She said, “Even though you are not married to Krsna, and even though you are all the wives of other men, still you have enjoyed so many nice, sweet pastimes with Krsna as His paramours. Why don't you just continue to enjoy this relationship with Him, instead of marrying Him and becoming His wives?”

“By pursuing your relationship with Krsna as His paramours, ” Paurnamasi explained, "you have all become more fortunate. It is due to this special relationship that you have received so much love from Krsna and have been able to make Him your own. The queens of Dvaraka are married to Krsna, but still that marital relationship has not completely satisfied Him.”

“Respected mother! ” Radharani replied, “What you are saying is true. But within our hearts we have a strong desire to marry Krsna and serve Him in the mood of His queens. We want to stay always with Krsna. We have been separated from Him for so long. We do not want to be separated from Him one moment longer. That's why we want to marry Him, so that we can stay with Him always, without any separation. " Having spoken. Radharani began to cry, shedding tears profusely. Her sakhis also wept, and together they shed so many tears that the ground at their feet became muddy.

Paurnamasi and Vrnda-devi had been with Krsna just before coming to meet Radharani and Her sakhis, so they both knew Krsna’s true desire. Paurnamasi said, “At a suitable time. Krsna will marry you all. But first I must reveal the truth to the Vraja-vasis the truth about your husbands.”

Radharani wiped the tears from Her eyes and asked, “What about our husbands?”

But Paurnamasi would not say anything further about that. Instead, she said, “At a suitable time, everyone will know that Krsna only is the eternal husband of Vraja's cowherd girls. After all, you gopis have given your hearts to Krsna only and to no one else. Your love for Krsna is great and pure, and that love will surely unite you with Krsna. So you should not cry anymore.”

Having heard these words from Paurnamasi, the gopis stopped crying and instead spoke among themselves as follows, ‘‘We are already married. How can our marriages be invalid? How can we be remarried to Krsna? How is it possible?” Though they could not understand it, still they had faith in the words of Paurnamasi due to their respect for her.

With folded hands, Sri Radha prayed, “O goddess! We hope your words will come true and we will find true happiness. All of us gopis have long been suffering, like Sita-devi suffered while confined by Ravana in the Asoka forest, or like Rukmini was suffering due to fear of Sisupala. In the same way, we are all suffering by being confined in the homes of men who are not Krsna. And yet we promise, just like Sita-devi, who as Ravana's captive was still never touched by Ravana, we loo have been slaying in the homes of our husbands, though we have never been touched by our husbands, even in our dreams. Krsna is the only man who is allowed within our minds, no one else.”

The gopis continued to talk among themselves, “Krsna has said His devotees that, although they may be separated from Him for some time, they will at last be re-united with Him. Great personalities have said that one who waits will receive Krsna's mercy. In addition, some great personalities have said that the gopis are the most eligible for receiving Krsna’s mercy. But the question remains: How will this happen? "

All the gopis, along with Radharani, asked Paurnamasi, “We have faith in your promise that we will get Krsna's mercy. He will surely fulfill our desires. But how will it be possible for Krsna to marry us, as we are already married? ”

Paurnamasi replied, “Just have patience for a little while longer. Very soon you will know everything. For the time being, do not tell anyone about what I have told you. But be assured that very soon you will attain the mercy of Sri Hari and your suffering will come to an end. Meanwhile, throw this letter into the Yamuna. You need not contemplate taking such extreme action. ”

Very soon thereafter Krsna returned to Gokula after a long absence. Every- one was happy, even happier than they were before Krsna had left for Mathura. At that time there had been constant fear that Krsna would be attacked by demons. But now all the demons were dead. There was no longer any threat to Krsna’s safely. So the Vraja-vasis were enjoying Krsna’s company without any fear that demons would come to attack their beloved. Krsna’s return had filled them with new life, and yet there was still one great danger that sometimes filled them with fear: that Krsna might one day return to Dvaraka. That would surely plunge them into the depths of separation again. Indeed, Krsna’s chariot, which stood in front of Nanda Maharaja’s palace, was a constant reminder that He could leave for Dvaraka at any moment. The prospect of His departure was truly frightening, especially to Nanda Maharaja and Mother Yasoda, who hoped that Krsna would never leave Vrndavana again. Mother Yasoda said to Nanda Maharaja. "You must make arrangements to keep Krsna here in Vrndavana. Please do not allow Him to go back to Dvaraka. If He goes back to Dvaraka, then my life will be finished. ”

One day. Nanda and Yasoda went to Krsna's room together. Krsna offered his respects to His parents by offering obeisances and then sat between them. Both Nanda and Yasoda each caught hold of one of Krsna's hands. Maharaja Nanda said. "My beloved son! Since You have come back to Vrndavana, we have regained our lives." And Mother Yasoda said, “We have come out of the darkness of sorrow. We are feeling unlimited happiness due to Your presence.”

Krsna replied. "I am also feeling happy to see that all of you are finally so happy.”

Nanda Maharaja said. “Though we all feel very glad to have You here, still we have one thing of which we are very much afraid - Your obligation to Dvaraka and to the Vrsnis, for whom You may one day need to leave Vrndavana. ''

“O father! mother! ” Krsna said. "I will never leave Vrndavana again. I have no such desire.”

“You may not desire to go back, ” Mother Yasoda said, “but it is the desires of others, and circumstances beyond Your control, that may one day force You to leave Vrndavana. If some king becomes inimical or war again starts between the Kurus and the Pandavas or Your wives and sons want You to return them, won’t You go back to Dvaraka then? ” Having voiced her fears, Yasoda started sobbing, all the while kissing and caressing Krsna. She was not pacified by Krsna's assurances that He would never again leave Vrndavana.

Nanda Maharaja said, “ If You want to give real pleasure to all of us Vra- ja-vasis, then You have to do something practical, something more convincing than just giving us Your assurance. You must do something that will leave no doubt You really intend to stay here and never leave Vrndavana.”

“O father! ” Krsna said. “Tell me immediately what I should do for you.”

Mother Yasoda said. “There is only one thing You can do to assure us. You must marry a girl from Vraja and settle Your family here, following the tradition of the gopas. ”

Immediately Nanda Maharaja agreed, “Yes! You are the prince of the cowherds, This is Your duty. By doing as Your mother has suggested You will convince us that You are telling the truth. That is the only thing that will give us peace. ”

Having spoken, both Nanda and Yasoda stared at Krsna with such pitiful looks on their faces that Krsna couldn't tolerate it. Finally, after a long silence. He replied, “Yes. I will follow your order. I will get married here in Vraja, to fulfill your desire. ” Mother Yasoda. so greatly relieved, shed tears of joy.

“Please, mother. Don’t cry. Tell me when and to whom I should be married. I will do exactly as you order.” Nanda and Yasoda embraced Krsna in great happiness, and Krsna felt very much pleased to see His parents so happy.

Then Nanda and Yasoda left Krsna's quarters. And Krsna began to wonder how His promise would be fulfilled. How would Yogamaya arrange this?

In the meantime, Balarama, accompanied by Mother Rohini, arrived in Vrndavana. They were very nicely received by the Vraja-vasis, and Nanda Maharaja was overjoyed to see Krsna and Balarama together in his home once again. He thought, “How fortunate I am! My sons have returned and have again taken up residence in my home. " When Krsna and Balarama first left Vrndavana for Mathura and then Dvaraka, only Rohini had accompanied Them. But as soon as she returned to Vraja, she immediately went to Yasoda’s kitchen to resume her service. She gave Radharani instructions to cook for Krsna and thus took responsibility for arranging the Lord’s morning and evening meals. By this time Uddhava also had reached Nanda Maharaja’s palace. Uddhava, as Krsna’s counselor, had been responsible for arranging Krsna’s many marriages in Dvaraka. He felt his counsel was again needed for Krsna’s marriage in Vraja.

Long ago the Vraja-vasis had built a hut for Paurnamasi on the bank of the Yamuna. From there Mother Paurnamasi would direct the great drama of Krsna’s pastimes. One day shortly after Krsna’s return to Vraja, Nanda Maharaja and Mother Yasoda went to meet Paurnamasi at her hut. Paurnamasi warmly greeted Krsna’s parents and gave them places to sit. She asked about the welfare of Krsna and Balarama. Paurnamasi said, “O king of Vraja! I known of your desire to keep Krsna in Vrndavana by getting Him married and thus bind Him in the ropes of family life. I also know that out of His love for you Krsna has agreed to your proposal. "

Nanda and Yasoda were astonished to hear this. How had she come to know about their plan? Paurnamasi asked them to disclose to her which cowherd girl they wanted their son to marry. Yasoda replied, “O goddess! I want my son to marry those young gopis headed by Dhanya who once performed severe austerity and worshiped Goddess Katyayani in order to marry my son. As these girls have never married, they will be most suitable. ”

Paurnamasi blessed Yasoda's proposal but added. “They are indeed suitable to be married to Krsna as they are very chaste and dedicated to Sri Hari. But what about Srimati Radharani and Her circle of gopis? Why don’t you consider them? ”

Nanda and Yasoda were bewildered by Paurnamasi's suggestion. They knew Radha and Her friends were very attached to Krsna. But they were all married. How could Krsna marry the wives of other men? Though they accepted Paurnamasi's words as true as the words of the Vedas, still they didn't understand how such a thing could be possible.

Nanda Maharaja said, “If you say these Vraja-gopis, like Radha, will marry Krsna, that is all right with us. But how can Krsna marry others’ wives? ”

Paurnamasi then revealed to Nanda and Yasoda the truth about the gopis' marriages. She told them how, by her divine powers, she had created the illusion of the gopis’ marriages. She had created doubles of the gopis. Nanda and Yasoda listened to Paurnamasi’s explanation in great wonder, but they believed whatever she said. Paurnamasi told them, “Radha and the other gopis like Her are as pure as the unmarried gopis headed by Dhanya. There should be no hesitation over their marriage to your son. thinking they are already married.”

“Whatever you say must be true,” Nanda Maharaja declared. Still, he had a doubt: “But will the gopis parents agree to this proposal?”

Paurnamasi said, “You both know very well that all the gopis’ parents had a strong desire to give their daughters in marriage to Krsna. At that lime, I am the one who advised them not to marry their daughters to Krsna. And the reason I did so is this: If they had married Krsna, then Krsna would not have been able to leave Vrndavana to fulfill His mission of killing all the miscreants. But now Krsna's mission has been fulfilled. Now Krsna and rge gopis can be married, which will bring us all great Joy. The illusion of the gopis’ previous marriages and their so-called husbands was all created by me.

“Even if the gopis' parents accept this proposal, ” Yasoda asked, “what about the rest of the Vraja-vasis? How will they feel about this arrangement? Will they believe that these girls are really unmarried and that it is their reflections who are serving their husbands? Will they accept that these girls have never personally served their so-called husbands? "

this Nanda Maharaja added, “Yes, whatever doubts might arise in the minds of the Vraja-vasis should be cleared. Otherwise, they will gossip about the impropriety of this arrangement, and the plans will be spoiled. This will greatly embarrass our son."

Paurnamasi re-assured Nanda and Yasoda, telling them not to be worried. She said, “Merely because Krsna is present, there can be no obstacle to this wedding. I shall also employ my own mystic power for this purpose. I propose that you request all the residents of Vraja to gather tomorrow in the village square for a public meeting. At that meeting I will do whatever is necessary to clear their doubts about Krsna’s marriage to Radha and the other gopis.”

By the order of Nanda Maharaja, a message was sent to all the villages of Vraja. All the prominent people of Nandagrama, Varsana, and Yavata were invited to this meeting.

The next day the citizens of Vraja assembled in the main square of Gokula. No one knew exactly what was the reason for the meeting. Maharaja Vrsabhanu and all of his family members came from Varsana. Abhimanyu and his family came from Yavata. Even the chieftains of Govardhana were there at the meeting place. Of course Nanda, Yasoda, Radharani and Her sakhis, and Krsna and Balarama and Their sakhas all were present there.

When everyone had finally arrived, Paurnamasi appeared, with Madhuman- gala, Nandimukhi, and a few other brahmanis. Nanda Maharaja respectfully welcomed Paurnamasi and her entourage and offered seats to all of them.

“O King! ” Paurnamasi began. "Are all of your invited guests here? ”

Nanda Maharaja replied. "Yes. Devil Everyone has arrived. ”

“Are Radha and Her gopi friends here? " Paurnamasi asked.

Nanda Maharaja confirmed their presence by pointing towards the area of the square where Radha and Her sakhis were standing.

“But I do not see all the gopis present here, ” Paurnamasi said. “In fact many prominent gopis are absent. Call all the Vraja-gopis to come here. Send some gopis to find out if any girls have been left behind in their houses. '’

Some of the senior ladies of Vraja immediately went to find out if any married gopis were still in their houses. After a short time those senior ladies returned to the meeting place looking very excited.

Paurnamasi asked, “Did you find any gopis still in their homes?”

One woman said, “What a surprise! When we went to the different houses Vraja’s cowherd men we found that their wives were all there engaged in different household duties. ”

Another woman said, “I saw Radha in the home of Abhimanyu. Yes, Just now I saw Her there, even though that same Radha is standing right here. ”

All the assembled villagers were surprised to hear what these ladies had found. As far as they could tell, all of the gopis in Vraja were there in the village square. How could they be somewhere else at the same time? One by one the elderly ladies who went to look for the Vraja-gopis gave their reports.

One woman said, “I saw Padma churning butter at her home. But she is here.”

Another woman said, “I saw Saibya cooking in her kitchen.”

Another woman said, ‘I saw Syamala cleaning her house just moments ago. But now I see she is here! "

Yet another woman said, "Bhadra was milking a cow in her husband's barn, but she is here too.”

Each lady testified that although this or that gopi was present there among the assembled Vraja-vasis, she had also been seen in her respective home engaged in this or that household duly. Everyone present there was amazed to hear these reports. By the order of Paurnamasi, the elderly ladies of Vraja again left the assembly. This time they went out into the homes of Vraja and brought all the young gopis back to the meeting place. Now there were doubles, two of each gopi. They were all looking at their doubles in a very puzzled way.

Two groups of gopis were now standing there - the original girls and their replicas. Everyone present at the meeting place was looking back and forth from one group of gopis to the other in great astonishment. They could not understand where the second group of gopis had come from. Actually, they could not be sure which group was the original girls and which group was the replicas.

Once everyone was completely bewildered, Paurnamasi sat down in a yogic posture and entered into deep meditation.

Paurnamasi was confident she could describe the mystery behind all these duplicate gopis. But she also knew that even if many, or most, of the Vraja-vasis could accept her explanation, some would not. And the doubts of a even few people would surely spoil the festive mood at Krsna’s wedding. So, in order to make her explanation even more convincing, she manifest her form as the personification of the Lord’s illusory energy, a form that is worshiped by people in general. Thus, through her meditation, Paurnamasi invoked the form of the goddess Yogamaya in front of the assembled Vraja-vasis. Everyone saw Maya-devi, in her transcendental eight-armed form, appear there in the center of the village square. She walked through the crowd, her feet never touching the ground, as is symptomatic of celestial beings. She appeared to each individual person simultaneously, as if she were standing directly in front of each one of them. Thus, when she spoke, each person felt as if the goddess were speaking personally to him or her.

Yogamaya said, first addressing Nanda and Yasoda, in whose home she had first appeared, “O Mother and Father and all Vraja-vasis! Please listen to me carefully and accept as truth whatever I am about to tell you. The marriages of Vraja’s young gopas and gopis never actually took place.. All these things were simply illusions I created in your dreams. By my power. I made all of you accept these dreams as reality. I did this with a very specific purpose in mind; so that later on the gopis could many Krsna. But because the gopis had to appear to be married. I created a replica of each one of them. The gopas, their so-called husbands, stayed with these duplicate gopis. The original gopis were never once touched by the gopas. This is not first time I have done this. It’s something I did during other yugas also. For instance, I created doubles of Sita. Samjna, and Rati. ”

To prove that what she said was true Yogamaya asked two groups of gopis to stand close to each other, a group of original gopis and a group of duplicates. Everyone could see that the two groups of gopis looked exactly identical to each other. While walking in between the two groups, Yogamaya continued. "I would reveal and or conceal one of these two groups of gopis according to whatever the situation required. During the rasa dance that took place during the sarat season, for instance, I manifested the gopis’ doubles. The original gopis went to meet and dance with Krsna, while the gopis’ doubles stayed with the gopas. The gopas knew that their wives wanted to go meet Krsna in response to the call of His flute, but as far as they knew, their wives stayed at home. ”

The Vraja-vasis were mystified to hear Yogamaya s explanation. At one point Yogamaya invited them to inspect the two groups of girls and see if they could find any difference between them. A few Vraja-vasis accepted Yogamaya s challenge, cautiously approaching one pair of gopis or another to inspect them more closely and try to understand whether there was any difference or not. Naturally, these gopis felt very uneasy to be the subjects of such an investigation and tried to cover their faces by pulling their veils more tightly over their heads. A few moments later Yogamaya asked each girl to stand next to her double -Radha stood beside the double of Radha, Lalita beside the double of Lalita, Candravali beside double of Candravali. and so on. Each pair of gopis were so much alike that even they themselves were bewildered by the resemblance, unable to understand who was the original and who was the replica.

Finally, Yogamaya withdrew her bewildering potency. All the Vraja-vasis could suddenly, and quite easily, distinguish the real gopis from their look- alikes.

The original gopis had pure love for Hari, and Hari alone. They had always thought of Krsna as their only husband. Due to their pure love for Krsna, their beauty was greatly enhanced, their eyes were shining, their bodies shone with a dazzling luster. The duplicate gopis did not have as much love for Krsna as did the original gopis. They did not exhibit the same qualities as did the original gopis. Moreover, the duplicates had accepted the gopas as their husbands. Thus all the Vraja-vasis could clearly see the difference between the real gopis and their doubles. They spoke among themselves, "O goddess! Moon shines at night. But when the sun rises in the sky, the moon loses it's luster. In the same way, we can now distinguish between the original gopis and their doubles by the differences in their luster and their other qualities. ” Now everyone was convinced by the words of Yogamaya. They all eagerly discussed the auspicious, and imminent, marriage of Krsna and the gopis.

Nanda Maharaja asked Yogamaya, “Now that we have two sets of gopis, where will these two groups of gopis go? ”

Yogamaya replied, “The original gopis should return to their parents' homes, and their doubles should go back to serving their husbands. When a suitable time arrives, you should make arrangements for the marriage of Krsna with the gopis, knowing that those girls were never married and were always pure. After saying this, Yogamaya offered her obeisances to Krsna and disappeared from the scene. All the Vraja-vasis, out of respect, offered her their obeisances and glorified her by shouting. “Jaya! Jaya! ”

By the order of Yogamaya. the parents of the original gopis prepared to take their daughters back home. At that time, Radharani said, “O citizens of Vraja! Now you have come to know that we were never married. We always loved Krsna, and Krsna only. It was our look-alikes who were married to their husbands and serving them very chastely. Yogamaya has explained all of this to you. Nonetheless, we have for some lime been known as women of poor character. Some of you have even called us unchaste, a disgrace to our families. We were known as kulakalankini. We suspect that even though Yogamaya has revealed the truth to you, still some may still believe the old rumors. We are afraid false perception of us will taint our relationship with Hari, and Hari s reputation for that matter. Therefore, before we go back to our parents’ homes, we want to prove to you that we are pure by undergoing a test by fire, just as Sita-devi once did in order to prove her chastity. That way we can eradicate all doubts, once and for all. To prove our purity, we will place our bodies into a blazing fire. If at any time we have deviated from pure love for Krsna, even for a moment, then let our bodies be burned to ashes. But if it is proven that we are faultless and that our love for Hari is indeed pure, then let all the cynics cast their doubts into the fire. ”

The Vraja-vasis were worried to hear Radharani speak like this. They could hardly bear it. Out of concern they approached Krsna and requested that He do something about it. Just at that time Durvasa Muni suddenly appeared there. The Vraja-vasis offered their obeisances to the sage and then told him about the gopis’ plan to prove their purity.

With a fierce look in his eyes, Durvasa Muni said, '‘Why do you doubt the gopis? I have told you so many times that Krsna is their only husband.”

“We have no doubt about this,” the Vraja-vasis said. “But the gopis have taken it upon themselves to perform this test of their purity.” The Vraja-vasis were overwhelmed fear for the gopis’ safety.

Durvasa Muni laughed and said, “All right. If the gopis are determined to be tested, then I will create a fire by my mystic power.” Having said this, Durvasa Muni yanked a hair from his head and threw it to the ground, which immediately created a fire so large that the flames were leaping up into the sky and so hot that the Vraja-vasis had to take several steps back in order to tolerate the blaze.

For the gopis, however, the fire was not at all hot; in fact, they felt that the flames were very cool and soothing. The Vraja-vasis were trying desperately to stop the gopis from stepping into the fire, but the flames were so hot that they could not get close to it.

Meanwhile, the gopis offered obeisances to Hari and, remembering Krsna within their hearts by keeping His holy name always on their lips, entered into the blazing fire one after another. Radharani was the first to enter, A few of the Vraja-vasis fainted at the sight. But within a few moments Radharani stepped out of the fire on the opposite side. Not a single part of Her body was burned!

After Radharani, all the other gopis followed in Her footsteps. Everyone could see that the gopis were completely untouched by the fire. They weren’t even affected by the smoke. Not a single hair or a thread of their garments had been burned, or even blackened. When the gopis came out of the fire they were looking brighter and more youthful. Just like this, all the gopis underwent this trial by fire. When the last gopi had finished her test, the fire was extinguished; in fact, it disappeared completely. Shortly thereafter, Durvasa Muni offered prayers to Srimati Radharani, bowed down to all the gopis, and then disappeared from there.

All those who had accused Radharani of impropriety, and who had doubted Her gopi companions, begged the girls for forgiveness and admitted their mistakes. The gopis were very respectful to these people as well, as they had no pride in their hearts. All the Vraja-vasis offered their affection to the gopis. And then those fortunate ladies returned to their respective parental homes.

The married gopis underwent a trial by fire and passed, proving themselves to be pure. After this they went back to their parents’ homes. And, after witnessing the trial of Radha and the other married gopis, the unmarried gopis headed by Dhanya also returned to their respective parents' houses.

Following tradition, there was an exchanges of gifts between the families of the gopis and Nanda Maharaja. In the meantime, Balarama's marriage was also arranged - He would be married to the gopis attached to Him. Mother Paurnamasi, with the help of an astrologer, fixed the date and time for the marriage. But Nanda Maharaja was worried. How could he arrange for the marriage of so many girls at one auspicious time? How could one Krsna accept the hands of so many gopis at once?

Madhumangala said. "O Nanda Maharaja! You need not worry. My friend Krsna and all His paraphernalia are identical. I will put His clothes, crowns, slicks, garlands, flutes, and foot dust as His proxies. " This made Nanda laugh.

Paurnamasi told Nanada, “Everything will be arranged by Yoga-maya. Krsna will be with all the gopis simultaneously." Nanad Maharaja was reassured by Paurnamasi’s words, and so he thanked her.

By the order of Purnamasi, priests applied tilaka to Krsna's forehead. And with this the auspicious marriage of Krsna and the Vraja-gopis began. On the second day there was an adhivas ceremony. Then, on the third day, Radha and Krsna were ceremonially bathed and dressed for Their wedding.

Gopal Champu describes the procession as follows, (translation by HH. Bhanu Swami)

"During the wedding procession Krsna spread Himself in all directions through His beauty. The sound of drums joined with the loud instrumental music, while jeweled horses, elephants, and chariots, pressing upon each other, jingled with ail their bells. The mixture of music created bewilderment in heaven and on earth. Please understand that there was nothing except the mixture of musical instruments and chanting of praises in heaven and on earth. The flowers thrown by the people mixed with the flowers showered from heaven. One could not tell them apart. In the procession Vaijayanti garlands were like fluliering flags. Experts in heavenly music played the music. The dancers were skillful in dancing and singing. Priests walked in front. Unlimited carts moved in front with the elders. His friends walked on either side. Servants walked while holding camaras. Balarama and Sndama were joyful along with Uddhava. The horses Saibya, Sugriva, Meghapuspa, and Valahaka were yoked to the chariot. The camphor water of Krsna's beauty, spreading everywere, was intensely attractive. Daruka was on the chariot with Krsna. Persons holding the ingredients accompanied them. Various groups of craftsmen followed along. Jesters appeared, creating comedy. The famous vandis praised the groom. Above, devatas headed by Brahma and Siva became absorbed in the festival. In the sky, persons engagd according to qualification and devatas showered flowers.”

Finally the procession reached Varsana.

All the other brides and their fathers arrived in Varsana in a big procession. They met the procession of Krsna and His entourage, creating great joy among the people of Vraja. Everyone was singing and dancing with great joy. The gopis were singing very nicely, and Madhumangala was dancing. All the brides along with Radha gathered in a big hall that belonged to King Vrisabhanu. Krsna appeared next to each of His brides, a miracle that Nanda Maharaja was surprised to sec, even though Purnamasi had told him this would happen so that Krsna could be married to all His brides during the same muhurta.

Shortly Krsna and the gopis exchanged vows, thus formally becoming husband and wife. Everyone present shed tears of ecstasy. Throughout that night the people of Vraja enjoyed grand celebrations with singing, dancing, and feasting. Then at dawn Nanda Maharaja invited the groom and His brides to return to Gokula. At the lime of their departure King Vrishnabhanu and his wife, Kirtida, instructed Krsna about how to protect and lake care of their daughter, Radha. Their voices choked up, their eyes full of tears, Vrishanbhanu and Kirtida placed Radha's hand in Krishna's and then finally bade Them farewell with great emotion.

Then the wedding parly, including Krsna and Radha and all the Vraja-gopis, returned to Gokula in a grand procession. This procession was much bigger than the previous one. Mother Yasoda had arranged a great festival to receive the Krsna and all His wives. She greeted her son and His new brides with great love and affection. Then Krsna introduced her, one by one, to all of His wives. Mother Yasoda embraced each and every one of them. But welcoming Radha in particular was a special delight for Mother Yasoda. She greeted Her with intense affection. The arrangements Yasoda had made for the festival were just as nice as the arrangements made for the previous festival at Vrishabhanu-pura. Yasoda received each new daughter-in-law as per tradition and assigned separate quarters for each one. Thus the dreams of Radha and Krsna and the gopis became reality.

(*This narration has been prepared in accord with similar narrations found in krsna-sangati by H. H. Sivarama Swami and Gopala-campu by Srila Jiva Goswami For more detailed accounts of these pastimes, readers are encouraged to consult die original texts.* )

## Section 2 - Tales of Srimati Radharani

## The Appearance of Kusuma Sarovara

One clay in the beautiful forest of Vrndavana, in the presence of their beloved Radha and Krsna, the gopis were collecting flowers in a basket for the Divine Couple. Due to a commotion, at one point the flowers fell from the basket to the ground, whereupon Radharani asked Krsna, “What should we do with all these flowers that have fallen? "

In reply, Krsna said, "O,dear Radhe! I would like to rinse these Rowers and use them to decorate Your hair."

Radharani was pleased with this idea. With Her permission Krsna then gathered all the blooms from the ground and was preparing to go to the Yamuna River to wash the Rowers. The Yamuna was at a far distance and it would take some time to reach it. so Radharani said, “We do not have time to go so far to the Yamuna. "

Krsna understood the mood of Radharani. She did not want Him to leave Her for so long. Krsna therefore took His flute and pierced the earth with it, creating a hole. Immediately it filled with water and gradually developed into a pond of clear water. Then Krsna washed the Rowers in the water of this pond.

Krsna combed and braided Radharani’s hair and then decorated it with the flowers. While Krsna was decorating Her hair, Radharani held a mirror in Her hand in such a way that She could sec Krsna's face in the mirror. In this way, Radharani enjoyed the darsana of Krsna while He enjoyed styling Her hair with the flowers.

As Krsna created this pond to wash the flowers (kusuma), this body of water is known as Kusuma Sarovara.

## The Gopis Steal Krsna’s Blanket

One day Krsna went alone to the forest to graze the cows. He strongly desired to meet with Radharani, so He played on His flute to attract Her. Radharani, along with her sakhis, thus came to the forest to be with Krsna.

However, before the gopis arrived Krsna’s cows wandered far away. Krsna went to search for His missing herd. When the gopis reached the forest they realized that Krsna had gone looking for His cows, but had left behind His blanket. Wanting to have some fun, They quickly hid it. When Krsna returned. He looked for His blanket but couldn't find it anywhere.

When Krsna questioned the gopis, some said, “A cow has eaten Your blanket. ”

Others replied, “If You dance, then we will find Your blanket for You. ”

Krsna went back to His house and complained to His mother, Yasoda.

A great poet devotee, Suradasa, describes this in a song as follows:

*maiya meri kamara cora lai*  
*main bana jata caravana gaiya suni dekha lai*  
*eka kahe kanha ten kamara jamuna jata bah*  
*eka kahe kanha teri kamara surabhi khaya gai*  
*eka kahe naco mere age lai dehumju nai*  
*suradasa jasumati fee age amsuvana dhara bahi*

“Maiya—dear mother! I went to the forest to graze the cows, but They had wandered far away. So I put down My blanket and went to search for them. While I was looking for My cows some sakhis stealthily stole My blanket. When I returned from searching for my cows, I could not find My blanket anywhere."

“Then I asked the sakhis, ‘Where is My blanket? If you have taken it, then return it to Me. That is My blanket.'

“One sakhi said, ‘Kanhaiya \[Krsna\]! I saw a cow eating Your blanket.'

“My dear mother! Please tell Me how could a kind-hearted cow eat My blanket? ''

“Another sakhi said, ‘Kanhaiya! If You dance in front of me, then I will give You a new blanket.'

“Maiya! These sakhis are teasing Me in so many ways."

While saying this, Kanhaiya’s eyes filled with tears. Mother Yasoda lifted up her son, Kanhaiya, and started feeding Him her breast milk.

## Ananta-sayi Visnu

One-day Sri Krsna was spending lime with Srimati Radharani and her sakhis. They were all engaged in sweet pastimes while discussing different topics. During their discussion they started to talk about Ananta-sayi Visnu. Krsna mentioned to Radharani and Her sakhis that He is non-different to Ananta-sayi Visnu.

Srimati Radharani and Her sakhis wanted to see the Ananta-sayi form of Krsna, and in order to fulfill their desire Krsna revealed this form. All the sakhis were astonished to see Krsna immersed in the bhava of Ananta-sayi Visnu, lying down on the milk ocean, with Srimati Radhika in the mood of Laksmi devi, massaging His lotus feet.

In his Vraja-vilasa-siaya (sloka 91), Sri Raghunatha Dasa Gosvami stales:

*yasya stimac-caram-kamale komale komalapi*  
*sri-radhoccair nija-sukha-krte sannayantl kucagre*  
*bhitapyarad atha nahi dadhaty asya karkasya-dosat*  
*sa sri-gosthe prathayatu sada sesa-sayi sthitim nah*

**“Although Srimati Radhika has extremely soft limbs, when She brought Sri Krsna’s very soft and charming lotus feet to Her chest. She could not bring Herself to place His lotus feet there. She felt that the touch of Her hard breasts might cause Him pain. May I obtain residence in the enchanting gostha \[Braja\] of Sesa-sayi Krsna. ”**

## Raining Flutes

Sri Krsna used to meet with Radharani from lime to time. Jatila and Kutila, the mother-in-law and sister-in-law of Radharani, respectively, were very much against these meetings. Kutila always watched Radharani closely and tried her best to catch Krsna whenever He was with Radharani.

One lime, when Sri Krsna was with Radharani in her bedroom, Kutila heard Krsna's voice coming from inside the room. She quickly ran towards Radharani s bedroom to try and catch Them together, but Krsna understood that Kutila was coming so He immediately escaped through the bedroom window. Kutila fell very disappointed knowing that Krsna had escaped and that she was not able to catch Him and prove to others that He had visited Radharani.

When Kutila entered Radharani's bedroom, she found Krsna's flute left on Radharani s bed. which He had accidently left behind when He had suddenly left the house. When Kutila found the flute she was elated, because now she could prove to the others that Krsna was with Radharani.

Kutila was very angry with Radharani, and Radharani started to cry in fear. Holding the flute with one hand. Kutila caught hold of Radharani with her other hand. She called all the villagers to show them Krsna's flute that she had found on Radharani s bed. Thus she tried to prove to everyone that Radharani meets secretly with Krsna. Radharani was feeling very embarrassed and was crying profusely because of it.

At this time, it just so happened that Purnamasi arrived there and inquired what all the commotion was about. Kutila described the whole incident of Krsna's meeting Radharani and how He had left His flute in Radharani's bedroom. Hearing this Purnamasi started to laugh and laugh. Kutila became very angry to see Purnamasi laughing so much.

She asked, “Why are you laughing? This is such a serious matter. Instead of taking it seriously you are laughing, ”

Purnamasi said, “Kutila! Don’t you know that last night it was raining flutes all over Vrndavana? There must have been flutes raining down over your house too. You found a flute in Radharani's bedroom not because Krsna left His flute there, but because there are flutes all over Vrndavana because it was raining flutes last night.”

Then everyone went to check Kutila's house and sure enough they found three flutes there. Then everyone went to check Jatila s house and found six flutes on Jatila's bed.

Thus, no one believed that Krsna had come to Radharani’s bedroom. Radharani was free from embarrassment and Kutila was made to feel a fool.

## Sri Krsna’s Fun with Srimati Radharani

One day Radharani and Krsna planned to have a swing pastime in the forest. With this idea in mind. They both arrived under a banyan tree. They were alone as Radha’s sakhis had not arrived yet. Before the arrival of the other sakhis, and just to pass the time, Sri Krsna decided to have some fun with Radharani.

Sri Krsna hung a swing from the branch of the banyan tree and then requested Radharani to climb onto the swing. Krsna purposefully hung the swing on the tree very high so that Radharani was not able to climb on it. Krsna came forward and picked up Radharani to help Her climb up on the swing.

On the pretext of helping Her, Krsna pul His hands around Radharani and embraced Her. Right at this lime the sakhis arrived and started to laugh and Joke when they saw what Krsna had done. In this way all the sakhis, along with Radha and Krsna, enjoyed the fun.

## Gopisvara Mahadeva

Mahadeva is known as the greatest Vaishnava and is very fond of serving Lord Sri Krsna. Once when the rasa-lila pastime of Lord Krsna with the gopis was about to lake place, Mahadeva developed a desire to watch the dance. Being very eager with this desire in his heart, Mahadeva travelled to Vrndavana but was stopped by some gopis at the entrance.

During the rasa-lila no other male person except Lord Krsna is allowed to be present. Mahadeva was very determined to enter Vrndavana to witness the rasa-lila and inquired how he might be allowed to see Krsna dancing with Radharani and all the other gopis. The gopis advised him that he should worship Purnamasi devi. Only by her mercy can one enter into the arena of the rasa-lila.

Following the advice of the gopis, Mahadeva worshiped Paurnamasi devi in order to please her and gain her permission to observe the rasa-lila.

Purnamasi devi was pleased with Mahadeva’s worship and look him by the hand to a nearby pond known as ‘Brahma kunda. ' After Mahadeva dipped into this kunda, he was transformed into a beautiful young gopi. Purnamasi devi arranged for this gopi form of Mahadeva to sit inside a kunja located at the northeast side of the rasa-lila area.

At the proper lime, Radha and Krsna came to the rasa-lila arena, and the sweet pastime of the rasa-lila with all the gopis began. The gopis did not feel as much happiness singing and dancing in the rasa-lila as usual and they wondered what could be the reason. They felt that perhaps some outsider, who was not qualified, had entered into this area of the rasa-lila pastime.

“For this reason maybe we are not feeling as much happiness as usual, " they thought.

The gopis then started to search out this unqualified person who may have entered the rasa-lila arena. They discovered Mahadeva in his gopi form sitting in one corner watching the rasa-lila. They did not recognize the gopi and realized she must be someone new.

They inquired from Mahadeva in his gopi form, “What is your name? Which village are you from? What is your husband's name? Who is your father-in-law? "

Mahadeva could not answer all the questions since he had not taken birth as a gopi in Vraja.

Yoga-maya had neither arranged her birth from the womb of a gopi, nor given her any name. This new gopi was not married to any gopa either, so what answers could she give? Not being able to provide any answers, Mahadeva in his gopi form just started to cry helplessly.

When Mahadeva could not give any answers, the gopis started to slap him on his checks. They slapped him so much that Lord Siva's cheeks became swollen. Purnamasi was a witness to all this as it happened and, seeing Mahadeva in this miserable condition, felt very sympathetic. She went and explained to all the gopis how this particular gopi was there because of her special mercy. She explained to them how it was Lord Siva in the form of a gopi and requested the gopis and Krsna to be merciful to her.

Sri Krsna, knowing the internal desire of Purnamasi, gave Sankara the name Gopisvara and then allowed her to witness the rasa-lila. Krsna granted Mahadeva a boon that any devotee who wishes to enter Vrndavana, especially into the sweet pastimes of Krsna, must receive the mercy of Gopisvara Mahadeva.

## Sri Krsna Applied Kajal to Srimati Radharani

*rasera avese krsna anjana laiya*  
*dilena radhikanetre maha harsa haiya*  
*(Bhakti-ratnakara)*

**“Krsna, being absorbed in transcendental mellows (rasa), applied anjana \[kajal\] to Radhika’s eyes with great pleasure. ”**

This is a sweet pastime between Krsna and Radharani, where Krsna Himself anoints the eyes of Radharani with kajal, or black eye ointment used to protect the eyes and enhance their beauty. This pastime took place at the home of Visakha sakhi.

Once Radharani was sitting in Visakha sakhi’s kunja, surrounded by Her other gopi friends. All the sakhis were happily decorating Radharani with various kinds of dresses and ornaments. Nearing the end of decorating Radharani with all these ornaments and cosmetics, only Her eyes still needed to be decorated with kajal.

Just at the moment the sakhis went to apply the kajal to Radharani’s eyes, they heard the sound of Krsna's flute. As soon as the sound of Krsna's flute entered Radharani’s ears. She became overwhelmed with a desire to meet with Him. Unable to control Herself, Radharani immediately left the kunja and ran to meet Krsna, not waiting for even a second to allow the sakhis to apply the kajal to Her eyes. Thus Radharani reached Krsna without having any kajal applied to Her eyes.
