As soon as Krsna saw Radharani, for whom He was waiting so eagerly. He picked Her up and placed Her on a very beautiful bed of flowers. He wrapped His hand around Radharani's neck and looked at Her beautiful face. Krsna immediately noticed that while Her whole body was very nicely decorated, Her eyes were devoid of any kajal.

He inquired from the sakhis the reason for the missing kajal, whereupon they replied, "We were decorating Radharani and we were almost finished except for applying kajal on Her eyes. Right at that moment She heard the sound of Your flute. She immediately left even though we requested Her to wait and finish being decorated. Radharani was so eager to sec You that She could not control Herself to wait for even one minute. ”

Krsna was very moved to hear about the loving affection Radharani had for Him. He asked the sakhis for the kajal, and with great love and affection applied the kajal on the eyes of Radharani with His own hands, Krsna held a mirror in front of Radharani so that She could see Her face. While Radharani enjoyed seeing Her eyes being anointed with kajal by Sri Hari, at the same time Sri Hari enjoyed seeing Radharani’s beautiful face reflected in the mirror.

## Krsna as Vanamali, the Forest Gardener

One day Radharani, along with Her sakhis, went to the forest to colled flowers. When they had a sufficient amount of flowers for the day, the sakhis suggested it was time to go back to Varsana. Radharani did not want to go back so early. She wanted to slay and search for Krsna in the kunjas. With this plan in mind, Radharani, along with all the gopis, went to Kusuma vana to search for Krsna.

Krsna is known as Govinda, Madhava and Syamasundara. All the sakhis know Him by these names but they didn't know Krsna is also known as Vana in all. 'Vana' means forest or garden and ‘mali' means one who takes care of a garden. Therefore Vanamali means 'forest gardener. ' When Radharani and the gopis went searching for Krsna, Krsna all of a sudden appeared from a thorny grove in the disguise of a gardener.

Krsna asked. "O, thieves! What are you all doing here stealing flowers from My garden? Don't you know I am Vanamali, caretaker of this garden? How dare you try to steal flowers from My garden without My permission! "

All of the gopis, including Radharani, were surprised to see Vanamali, the gardener. Radharani was very confused to sec Krsna in this form.

The sakhis inquired, “Who are you? How can You dare call us thieves? "

Krsna replied, ‘I am Vanamali, the caretaker of this forest garden. This is my forest. ”

After listening to Vanamali, Lalita and Visakha understood that this forest gardener was none other than Krsna. They both glanced sideways at each other and kept quiet.

The sakhis then introduced Radharani, saying, “Do you know Her? This is Sri Radha. She is the queen of this forest. That’s why She is known as Vrndavanes- vari. She is the mistress of this forest. This forest belongs to Her. ”

Vanamali replied, “These flowers are the result of My hard work in this forest. I have full ownership over these flowers. ”

In this way a quarrel started between Vanamali and Radharani’s sakhis. Radharani did not recognize Krsna disguised as Vanamali. She looked bewildered and remained silent. Finally, at one point Radharani saw a flute tucked into the cloth at Krsna’s waist and She then recognized Him.

She cried out, “There is no use arguing with Him. He is not Vanamali; He is Vanavihari, one who moves to enjoy in the vana or forest! ”

After hearing this from Radharani, all the sakhis started to laugh out loud, upon which Krsna bowed down His head. Then the sakhis removed the disguise from Krsna and He, the Lord of the universe, assumed the mood of a humble servant.

## Krsna Receives Special Alms from Radharani

Once there was a time when Radharani had developed mana towards Krsna. For this reason. She did not meet with Him for quite some time. Due to separation from Radharani, Krsna was feeling very restless and wanted very much to meet with Her. He consulted with Visakha to figure out a solution to His problem.

Visakha advised Krsna to dress like a brahmana boy and in disguise go to Radharani's house to beg alms. Accordingly, Krsna dressed Himself like a brahmana boy with a sacred thread across His shoulder and wooden shoes on His feet. Carrying a begging bowl, a danda in His hands, and a book under His arm. He arrived at the door of Jatila s house in javala.

It was early morning when Krsna started to beg alms at jatila’s door and neither Jatila nor Kutila had taken their bath. They were busy making cow dung patties and were not clean enough to properly offer alms. Jatila called for Radharani and requested Her to offer alms to the young brahmana boy, but Radharani refused to go before any male person other than her husband, Abhimanyu.

Since receiving the alms was delayed. Krsna in the disguise of the young brahmana beggar, said to Jatila, "Dear Mother! It is gelling late for Me. I don't stay at one place more than the time it takes to milk a cow. I have been waiting here for so long now; I have to leave whether I get alms or not. ”

Jatila became very worried hearing that the young brahmana would leave their house without receiving any alms. She knew this would bring inauspiciousness to their family and would create a disturbance in the house. Someone in their family could die or one of the cows might die. With great haste Jatila entered the house and insisted Radharani go out and offer alms to the brahmana boy. Jatila repeatedly requested Radharani until She finally agreed. Then Jatila left to continue making cow dung patties.

Radharani prepared a plate of alms with different ingredients such as grains and vegetables and went to the door, along with Her sakhis, to offer the aims to the brahmana boy.

Krsna, in the disguise of the brahmana beggar, said, “I do not require alms of this sort. I have come here to beg from You a different kind of alms. I want Your mana towards Me. ”

Radharani then understood that this boy was Krsna in the disguise of a brahmana boy who came to beg. She uncovered Her veil to show Her smiling face and poured on Krsna’s head everything She had brought on the plate to give Him as alms.

Krsna was very happy to accept these alms. As He left Jatila’s house. He meditated on the special alms He received from His beloved Radharani.

## Kutila Criticizes Radharani

Jatila and her daughter, Kutila, play a very special role in enhancing the sweetness of the pastimes of Radha and Krsna, even if it apparently looks like they are a hindrance to Their meeting.

One day Krsna had gone to meet Radharani at Abhimanyu's house in Javata. There, inside Radharani's room, Krsna was alone with Her. Some how Kutila, the daughter of Jatila, saw Krsna and Radharani meeting alone inside the room. While Krsna and Radharani were inside the room Kutila went there and very quietly locked the door from the outside. Kutila was very confident that today she would prove how Radharani meets with Krsna alone.

For this purpose, after locking the door from outside, she started to shout loudly. Upon hearing her shouting, Jatila, Abhimanyu and his younger brother, Durmada, gathered at the spot. Kutila informed them that she saw Krsna and Radharani meeting inside the room and that she locked the door from the outside. All of them opened the door with great curiosity but to their surprise they saw that Krsna was not present. Instead Radharani was worshiping a Kali deity.

Upon seeing this, everybody came to understand that Kutila was simply envious of Radharani and schemed to give Her a bad name. She falsely accused Radharani who in reality was innocent. They all chastised Kutila for falsely accusing Radharani. Kutila felt very bad and sat there with a long and dark face.

**Radharani Enjoys Sri Krsna’s Darsana**

Srimati Radharani used to be invited to Mother Yasodas house to cook wonderful preparations for Sri Krsna. Radharani was blessed by Durvasa Muni that not only would Her cooking be extremely tasty, but whoever ate Her cooking would be benedicted with good health and a long duration of life. For this reason. Mother Yasoda requested Radharani to come to her house daily and cook for Krsna.

One day Radharani and Her sakhis were on their way to Nanda Maharaja’s house to cook for Krsna. From a distance, they saw Krsna and His sakha friends milking cows. Lalita sakhi said, ‘‘Just see! There is Krsna, a crown-jewel among the debauchees. He is milking a cow but actually He is looking at us with greedy eyes. If we go through this way. He will not spare us from leasing. It is better if we walk some other way and avoid this path. ”

When Radharani heard this She insisted, “Why should we go another way? We should go this way without any fear. Let us see what this debauchee can do to us. ”

While talking like this, Radharani, along with all Her sakhis, proceeded towards the direction where Krsna was milking the cows. Krsna saw Radharani, along with Her sakhis, passing nearby. When Radharani was close enough Krsna directed the udder of the cow He was milking towards Radharani’s face and sprayed milk on Her. Radharani became drenched with the milk. All the saêhas of Krsna and the sakhis of Radharani began to laugh, enjoying the fun very much. Radharani showed some loving anger by raising Her eye brows as if She did not like it but actually She also enjoyed Krsna's prank.

After being drenched in milk, Radharani and Her sakhis then proceeded slowly along the path. Suddenly Her pearl necklace broke and all of Her pearls scattered all over the ground. Radharani started to pick up each pearl and, while picking up the pearls, took full advantage of the lengthy darsana of Krsna. Actually, on the pretext of collecting pearls. She delayed intentionally just to have more time enjoying seeing Krsna and Her sakhi friends understood this very well.

## Sri Krsna as the Snake Charmer

One day Srimati Radharani, along with Her sakhis, had gone to Phul- bari to collect flowers. While they were picking the flowers, Sri Krsna suddenly arrived.

He inquired from Radharani, “Who are you? Every day You come and steal flowers from My garden.”

Radharani replied, “Don't You know who I am? ”

Krsna started to slowly walk away while playing on His flute and glancing at Radharani. As soon as Krsna was out of Her sight Radharani fainted with a great feeling of separation from Krsna. Lalita sakhi, along with all the other sakhis, became very worried, thinking that a black snake had bitten Radharani. They tried their best to revive Her but failed.

Just at that lime Krsna appeared on the scene disguised as a snake charmer. Krsna acted as if He were chanting some mantra into Radharani's ear. Actually He was saying, "I am here. Open your eyes and see Me. "

As soon as Radharani heard Krsna's voice Her consciousness was immediately revived and She started to smile knowing Krsna was next to Her. All the sakhis became happy seeing this take place.

## Radharani Sees the Gauranga form of Krsna

One night Srimati Radharani and Krsna were sleeping in Nidhuvana. rewards the last part of the night Radharani had a wonderful dream. After remembering this surprising dream, Radharani woke up Krsna and told Him, “I had a very wonderful dream. I saw a very nice river with a very beautiful bank just like the Yamuna. I saw on the bank of that river one very wonderful golden-complexioned young man singing and dancing to the rhythm of mrdan- ga and karatalas. He was exhibiting great ecstasy.

“Being absorbed in an internal mood he was crying. Hey Krsna! Hey Radhe! Where are You? Where have You gone?'

“Sometimes he rolled on the ground and fell unconscious. He was distributing this mood to everyone in the universe freely, without considering who was eligible and who was not eligible. ”

Radharani continued, “Oh, My most dear! When I saw that golden Kisora form, I started to wonder, ‘Who is this golden-complexioned youth? Is it Me crying, ‘Hey Krsna! Hey Krsna! '? That youthful golden-hued boy was crying out loudly, ‘Hey Radhe! Hey Radhe! Where are You? Where have You gone? ’ Was this You, my dear Krsna? ”

Sri Krsna said, '', My dear most Radhe! You have seen Me at different times in My different forms. I have given You many opportunities to see My different forms such as Narayana and Sesa-sayi Visnu and so on. I never before saw You surprised to see these different forms of Mine. Now You look so bewildered at the sight this golden form of a boy. But I cannot say who is this golden boy. ”

Speaking like this Lord Krsna began to smile.

Srimati Radharani replied, “Oh, Lord of My heart! I am sure that this golden form is no one else but You, because who else except You can infatuate Me in this way? ”

Krsna then showed Radharani His Kaustabha jewel. When Radharani looked into the Kaustubha jewel She saw everything She had seen in Her dream.

Radharani said, “I have heard that Garga Muni told Nanda Baba that at some point in Kali-yuga You will appear as the golden-complexioned Gauranga. Garga Muni’s words do not go in vain, so I am sure during this coming Kali-yuga You will appear with a golden color, as Gauranga, and My dream will come true. This golden-complexioned kisora I saw in My dream is no one else but You. ”

Upon hearing this Sri Krsna agreed, “Yes, Your dream will come true. In this coming Kali-yuga I will accept Your golden complexion, and the sentiments of Your inner heart, and appear as Gauranga. I will accept Your mood to personally relish Your nectarine bhavas. At the same time, for the benefit of the people in general. I will preach raga-marga bhakti. People will develop love for Me by chanting the Hare Krsnamaha-mantra. ”

Through this pastime Krsna's appearance as Gauranga Mahaprabhu was predicted.

## Radharani blesses Visvanatha Cakravarti Thakura

“Visvanatha Cakravarti Thakura was the protector, guardian, and acarya during the middle period \[1650-1750\] of the historical development of Gaudiya Vaisnavism.” — Bhaltisiddhanta Sarasvati Thakura.

Once while compiling Mantrartha Dipika \[an explanation of the kama-gayatri\] Visvanatha became perplexed. According to his exhaustive research indicating twenty-five, he could not substantiate why Krsna Dasa Gosvami wrote in the Caitanya-Caritamrita that the kama-gayatri contains twenty-four and one-half syllables.

Sri Radhika instructed Visvanatha in a dream, “O Visvanatha! Please don’t lament anymore. What Krsna Dasa wrote is true. He is also My confidential maidservant, and thus he knows everything about My innermost moods. The Kama-gayatri is the mantra for worshiping Me. Indeed, I am revealed by the syllables of this mantra. Without My mercy, no one can understand the mystery of this mantra. ”

“Look in the book entitled ‘Varnagama-bhasvat' to find the solution to the half syllable. Krsna Dasa read this book and then wrote as he did. The letter ‘ya’ which is followed by the letter ‘vi’. as in the words kamadevaya vidmahe, is considered to be a half-syllabic. This falls on Krsna's forehead because His forehead is shaped like the halo of a half moon. All the other letters of the mantra are full syllables and therefore full moons. Now wake up, check that book, and compile this evidence for everyone's benefit.”

Instantly awaking, Visvanatha cried out in ecstasy, “Hey Radhe! Hey Radhe! Hey Radhe! ” Receiving Radharani’s darsana infused Visvanatha’s writing with divine sakti.

## Two Krsnas in the Kunja

Once at Radha-kunda Krsna desired to dress Radharani to look exactly like Himself. On Krsna’s order the manjaris smeared black aguru and kasturi \[deer musk\] all over Srimati Radharani’s golden body. Then they wrapped Syama’s yellow dhoti around Sri Radha and decorated Her with Krsna’s ornaments. They tucked Krsna’s flute into Radha's sash for the finishing touch.

Sri Radha, disguised as Krsna, sal facing north on a kusa grass asana covered with a tiger skin. Holding a set of rudraksa beads and looking like a great yogi, Sri Radhika sat silently meditating. Krsna sat beside Sri Radha on another asana. The sakhis became bewildered when they entered the kunja and saw two meditating Krsnas.

One sakhi said, “What land have we come to where there are two Krsnas? Just see. They are both Syama blue in colour, wearing flower garlands and peacock feathers in Their hair. ”

The sakhis were confused and especially worried. How would they explain the disappearance of Sri Radha to Jatila, Radha's mother-in-law? Thinking the ‘yogi’ with the rudraksa beads must be the real Krsna, the sakhis accepted the second Krsna to be Sri Radha.

They said, "Hey, Radhe! Why have You disguised Yourself to look like this lusty lover? Let Krsna sit on His tiger skin and chant mantras. We should leave here immediately! ”

Imitating Sri Radha's voice, Krsna said, ‘'O Lalita! I am loo embarrassed to tell you what Krsna did to make Me like this. If you come with Me to a private place I will reveal everything to you. ”

Hearing the familiar voice of Radha, the sakhis felt relieved that this was Radha. Upon touching the bodily limbs of Krsna, the sakhis said, “Except for Your voice. Your whole body perfectly resembles Krsna’s. How did this happen? ”

Continuing to imitate Radha’s voice Krsna said, “Well, Krsna used mantras to hypnotize Me. Then He chanted over palm-fulls of water which He rubbed all over My body. Amazingly, every part of My body became exactly like His. Since My mouth was closed, however, the mantra water did not enter my throat and affect My voice. I feel too shy in public to say what He did after that. ”

Understanding Radha’s feelings, all the sakhis except Lalita left the kunja. They knew that Lalita would later inform them of the secret. Then Krsna took Lalita to another private kunja. Once inside, Radha (Krsna) tightly embraced Lalita and began kissing her passionately. Startled, Lalita said, “Hey Radhe! What are you doing? ”

Continuing to imitate Sri Radha’s voice, the clever Krsna said, “Hey Lalita! I feel too embarrassed to tell you what Krsna did lo Me, so I will show you instead. ”

Then Krsna stopped imitating Radha's voice and began enjoying with Lalitasakhi to His full satisfaction, amidst great laughter and merry-making. Promising to keep Krsna’s secret, Lalita left the kunja and re-joined the sakhis.

Lalita told Visakha, “O sakhi, you need to enter this kunja and find out for yourself. ”

Krsna, the gallant lover, enacted the same pleasure pastimes with Visakha, Campakalata, Citra, and the other sakhis one after another.

## Kosi (Kosivana)

Kosi is located on the Mathura-Delhi highway about thirty-five miles from Mathura and some ten miles from Chatravana. It is the site of a wonderful pastime between Radha and Krsna.

Once Sri Krsna was so eager to meet Srimati Radharani that He knocked on Her door in the night. Prom inside the house, Srimati Radharani asked, "Ko'si? ” \[Who are you?\]

Sri Krsna replied, ”I am Krsna.”

The word ‘Krsna’ also means 'black snake’. Radharani took the meaning of Krsna as black snake and therefore said, “If You are a black snake, then why do You need to be here? Do You want to bite Me? Go to the forest. You have no purpose here. ”

Krsna said, “No. Priyatame! I am Ghanasyama.”

Ghana means ‘dark blue' and sometimes it means ‘dark cloud. Radharani look 'Ghanasyama' to mean ‘ dark black cloud'.

She said, “If You are a black cloud, then You are not needed here either. Don't shower rain here. It will make My courtyard muddy. Go to the forest and fields and pour rain over there. ”

Then Krsna, standing outside the door said, “Priyatame! I am cakri. ”

'Cakri' also means 'potter. ' The potter uses cakra or discs to make clay pots. That’s why a potter is also known as 'Cakri. 'Radharani therefore said, "There is no need for a cakri here. No wedding festival is being held in My house. Take your clay pots and go to where a marriage is being celebrated. ”

Krsna again pleaded saying, “Priyatame! I am Madhusudana. ”

‘Madhusudana' also means ‘bumblebee. 'Radharani intentionally took the word 'Madhusudana' to mean 'bumblebee’ and said, “O, You are a madhusudana—a bumblebee? If You are madhusudana, then immediately fly from here and go to a flowergarden to taste the rasa of the flowers. There is no flower garden here. ”

Sri Krsna again said, “Are! I am Your beloved Hari. ”

'Hari’ also means ‘monkey’ and ‘lion. 'Radharani, taking hari to mean monkey or lion, laughingly replied, “What is the need of a monkey or lion here? Do You want to scratch Me? You should quickly run away to a dense forest. We are all scared of lions and monkeys here. ”

Thus, Srimati Radharani joked with Sri Krsna in various ways.

As Srimati Radharani had asked Krsna, "Ko'si? \[“Who are You? ''\] when he had knocked on Her door that night, this place is therefore called 'Kosi' or ‘Kosivana. '

## Krsna’s Worship of Radha-kunda Pleases Radharani

One day Radharani and Sri Krsna were sitting within a beautiful kunja on the bank of Radha-kunda. At that lime Radharani saw Her reflection on the shining bluish chest of Krsna. Actually Radharani's golden effulgence was reflecting in the shining blue chest of Syamasundara. When Radharani saw what She thought was one of her rival gopis like Syamala or Candra embracing Krsna, Radharani became very angry to see this. She was not able to tolerate this at all. Immediately she turned her back on Krsna, covering her veil over her head and moved to another cottage.

The sakhis went to pacify Radharani. Radharani thought Krsna had sent them to appease Her.

Radharani told them, “I can tolerate this no longer. I came to meet this boy, giving up all my shame, Jeopardizing the honour and reputation of my family, and taking so much trouble and pain. Now I see He is enjoying with one of my rivals right before My eyes. This is unbearable. "

Whatever way the sakhis tried to pacify Radharani, she would not be pacified.

The gopis knew Krsna has no fault in this. Radharani has mistook reflection of her own as some other sakhi, thinking Krsna is enjoying with other sakhi.

Because the gopis had tried but failed to pacify Radharani, the gopis requested Krsna to go to Radharani and beg Her forgiveness, Following the order of the gopis, Krsna went to beg forgiveness from Radharani. But still she was not pacified. She would not speak to Krsna and sal there with Her head covered.

Thus Krsna failed to break the mana or spiritual anger of Radharani. “How to please Radharani? ” thought Krsna.

If He could please Radha-kunda, then Radharani would stop being angry with Him. Thinking like this, Krsna took bath in Radha-kunda and worshipped her by offering flowers moistened with His tears of love. Then He offered dandavats by praying for the mercy of Radha-kunda by which Radharani would be pleased with Him. In this way Sri Krsna taught all of us to worship Radha-kunda.

## Krsna as a Cowherd Girl

During a Vrndavana festival, all the gopis were decorating sanjis, except Radharani. Lalita and Visakha had returned to their villages to decorate their sanjis. Radharani said, “We are too late to decorate our sanjis. ”

Krsna appeared there in the disguise of a cowherd girl. He said, “You go to see everyone else’s sanji and I will decorate your sanji. ”

That day the cowherd community went to inspect the decorations of the sanjis in every house. After inspecting them all, they concluded that the sanji of Radharani, Lalita and Visakha was the best.

They asked these three gopis, “Your sanji is so beautiful—how could you decorate the sanji so nicely? “

Pointing towards Krsna in the disguise of cowherd girl they replied, “We had help from this new girl. ”

Everyone was pleased to see the blue-complexioned girl with a veil. No one could recognize that the girl was Krsna!

## Krsna’s Long Wait for Radharani

Sri Krsna very intensely desired to meet Radharani. He fell very’ restless and distressed due to separation from her. That evening, in order to meet Radharani, He went to Javata, He was waiting outside Jatila’s house under a berry tree. Climbing upon a branch of that tree, Sri Krsna started cooing like a kokila (black cuckoo).

Srimati Radharani and Her girlfriends understood that it was not the sound of a real kokila, but thought that it must be Sri Krsna signaling to meet with Radharani.

However, as soon as Krsna tried to enter the house, the vigilant Jatila, hearing a sound, would call out “K ai re? '" (“Who is there?”). Krsna would again hide in the bushes. This way throughout the night Krsna was trying to enter the house but was obstructed by Jatila. Thus, His attempts to meet with Srimati Radharani were repeatedly foiled. Krsna has to thus spend the whole night under the berry tree. Finally, being dejected, He gave up hope and left.

Sri Rupa Gosvami has described this incident in his book Ujjvala-nilamani (1.16):

*sanketa-krta-kokiladi-ninadamkamasa-dvisah kurvato*  
*dvaronmocana-lola-sankha-valaya-kvanam muhuh srnvatah*  
*keyam keyam iti pragalbha-jarati-vakyena dunatmano*  
*radha-prangana-kona-koli-vitapi-krode gata sarvari*

In this verse, one sakhi is describing Sri Radhakrsna’s paradhinala(depcnding upon others) of the previous night to her Radharani: “Last night, Sri Krsna was standing under a berry tree in Srimati Radharani's courtyard, cooing like a kokila again and again. Srimati Radharani understood His signal but as soon as She went to open the door. Her bangles and ankle-bells sounded. Sri Krsna also heard them. Then, from inside the house old Jatila would repeatedly call out, 'Who is there? ’

“Hearing her loud call, Sri Krsna, with a distressed heart, spent the whole night under that berry tree. ”

## Tamala-vana Pastime

Surrounded by a dense forest of tamala trees, this is a place where Sri Sri Radha-Krsna met and became immersed in nectar-filled loving pastimes.

One lime Rasika-Bihari Sri Krsna met with Radharani and the sakhis in this tamala kunja. Many kinds of creepers and vines had wound themselves around the tamala trees, embracing them, and had spread over the trees to create an exquisite kunja. Shri Krsna pointed to one creeper and asked Priyaji, ‘‘Why is this creeper winding itself around the tamala tree? "

Srimati Radhika smilingly answered, '‘It is the nature of the creeper to cover the tamala tree with its shoots, leaves, and flowers. Although this tree has no fruit or flowers of its own, its fortune is that the creeper enhances the tree’s beauty with it own leaves and flowers. ”

Just then a breeze caused the creeper to quiver, the sight of which immersed Kisora-Kisorun bhava.

## The Blessed Pilu Tree

Behind Visvanatha Cakravarti's bhajana-kuiira is a short pilu tree. Its dense foliage hangs down the steps of Manasa-pavana-ghata at Syama-kunda. It is said that this pilu tree was once a brahmana who has now taken this humble form to render the topmost service to Radhika and the gopis.

When Radha and Her sakhis bathe there at midday, they hang their clothes on this tree. Visvanatha Cakravarti would often discuss Radha-Syama's pastimes with this great personality. While offering some Syama-kunda water to this pilu tree devotee, we can beg for his mercy that we may someday serve in Radha-Madhava's pastimes.

“I eternally bow down to Vrndavana's trees and vines, which are far, far beyond the influence of matter, and which mercifully grant the goals of life to one who even sees, touches, hears about or remembers them. ” (VM)

## Umrao

This village is situated approximately four or five miles east of Chatravana. When the sakhis heard Sri Krsna’s proclamation, they complained to Lalita about Him. Bhakti Ratnakara describes what ensued:

*lalitadi sakhi krodhe kahe bara bara*  
*radhikara rajya ke karaye adhikdra*  
*aiche katha kahi lalitadi sakkhigana*  
*radhikare umrao kaila iksana*

**"Lalita became angry and said, 'Who is that person who dares to claim authority over Radhika’s kingdom? We will retaliate against Him. ’ ”**

Saying this, she sat Radhika on a beautiful throne and pronounced Her to be the undisputed Umaraha, or queen. (Umaraha means 'Lord of a kingdom.’)

Citra Sakhi held an umbrella over Radhika s head, and Visakha fanned Her with a camara. Lalita-ji sal on Radhika's left as Her minister of stale. One sakhi offered Her pan, and the remaining sakhis acted as subjects. Silting on Her throne, Radhika ordered the sakhis thus:

*mora rajya adhikdra kare yei jana*  
*parabhava kari tare ana ei ksana*

**“Go and defeat the person who desires to usurp My kingdom. Bind Him and bring Him before Me. '’**

Receiving the order of their Umrao, thousands and thousands of sakhis with flower-sticks in their hands left for battle. When Arjuna, Lavana, Bhrnga, Kokila, Subala and Madhumangala saw them approaching, they fled in all directions.

One clever sakhi, however, caught Madhumangala, bound him with her flower garland, and brought him to the lotusfeet of the Umrao. Some gopis slapped Madhumanga la's cheeks a few times and said, "What audacity you have to try to unlawfully seize the authority of this kingdom from our Umrao. We will punish you now. ”

Madhumangala bowed his head low like a defeated general and said, “That is befitting. We are defeated, but give such a punishment that will fill my stomach! ”

Hearing this, Maharani Radhika started laughing and said, “This is just some gluttonous brahmana. Release him. ”

The sakhis then gave them copious amounts of laddus and let him go.

Madhumangala then returned to Chatrapati Maharaja Krsna. Pretending to weep, he gave Krsna a detailed report of his humiliating detention.

Hearing this. Krsna together with Madhumangala and the sakhas invaded Umrao. When Srimati Radhika saw Her Prana-vallabha Sri Krsna, She became very embarrassed and wanted to lake off Her Umrao dress, but the sakhis, laughing, would not let Her do so.

Madhumangala sealed Chatrapati SriKrsna on Umrao Radhika's right side. They both made a treaty in which Krsna accepted Radhika's sovereignty. Madhumangala folded his hands before Srimati Radhika and said, “The kingdom of Krsna's body is now under Your rule. You can take whatever You desire from Him. ”

All the sakhis and sakhas became very blissful participating in this pastime.

This village is called Umrao after this Umrao-lila, and is also known as Radha-sthali. Afterwards Paurnamasi enthroned Radhika here as Vrajesvari, the Queen of Vraja. There is also a Kisori-kunda there, where Sri Lokanatha Gosvami performed bhajana. The worshipable Deities of Lokanatha Gosvami, Sri Radha-Vinoda, who appeared from this kunda, are now worshiped in Jaipur.

## Sri Radha Mana Bhanjana

One day Sri Krsna informed Radharani in advance to meet with Him at Sanket. On His way to meet Radharani, He saw Padma, a sakhi of Can- dravali. Padmasakhi came before Krsna in great ecstasy and blocked His path.

Padma said, “Krsna, You are very cruel. We all have become tired from searching for You all over the place. Still we could not find you anywhere. What shall I tell you about our dear sakhi, Candravali? By her sadness even stone melts. O, Vidagdha-nagara, please you should meet with Candravali for at least once and save her life. If you say that you do not have time now, then I will not let You proceed one step from here. "

Most clever Sri Govinda thought, “If I say I have no lime, then Padma will not let me go so easily. "

Externally he smiled and said, “O Padme! I am always happy with beautiful form of Devi Candravali. In these three worlds, there is no one who is so dear to Me like Candravali. My heart cries out as soon as I hear her name 'Candravali, ’ I think 'When will I see that Candravali? ' Padme! I should have gone with you now. but I have a royal invitation from Vrsabhanu-pura. Thus I am in great trouble. Not only that, but since this morning his servant has been coming to my house again and again to call me. Thai’s why now you must let me go to attend the royal invitation with Subala. Later on. I will come and meet with your dear sakhi, Candravali. Please pacify Candravali by informing her of my eagerness to meet with her. "

Speaking very politely, Krsna pleased Padma and was about to leave, but at that lime Candravali arrived along with all her sakhis. When Krsna saw Candravali, He look her inside the kunja with great pleasure along with her sakhis. There he engaged with Candravali in many pastimes. Candravali, holding Krsna's two hands with hers, said, “O, Vrajarajanandana, I have been wandering all over the forest keeping aside all my duties Just to have your darsana. Sometimes I gel lo see you and sometimes I do not see you. When I have the chance to see you, on that day four prahara passes quickly like one moment. I am simply living because of Your love.

Clever Krsna greeted Candravali sweetly, saying, 'Pranapriye! I have no language to speak to you. You just ask Padma. She will tell you about My eagerness for you. I wander all over the forest searching only for you. In the forest if someone just utters ‘Candra’ immediately I lose all my patience. When I sec the moon in the sky I am simply reminded of you. All my senses desire to associate with your body. Priye! I have now revealed my mind to you. ”

When Sri Krsna and Candravali were talking with each other like this, there was Radharani's parrot, Sarika, sitting on the branch of a tree and hearing everything. Sarika immediately went to the assembly of Radharani's sakhis and reported the conversation between Krsna and Candravali.

Lalita became very angry to hear that Sri Krsna had met with Candravali. She said to the other sakhis, “You all go to the house of Govardhana Malla (Candravali's husband) and inform him of everything. He should keep his wife confined inside. Then the sakhis went to inform Candravali's mother-in-law, Karala. She became very angry to hear this and went to the kunja of Candravali, making a roaring sound. Padma saw the old lady from a far distance and became very frightened. When she informed Candravali about the arrival of her mother- in-law Karala, Candravaliimmediaiely fainted in fear.

Krsna very affectionately said, “There is no fear. I am here to protect you. ”

Then immediately Krsna accepted the form of Katyayani. All the sakhis were very happy to see Krsna in that form.

In the mean time, Karala arrived and angrily said, “Now I have understood your mentality. You all are taking my daughter-in-law and wandering in the forest every day just to get the association of other persons. Today I will arrange a proper punishment to all of you for this. ”

Upon hearing these words from the old lady, Padma said, “Why are you saying these cruel sentences? We have brought your daughter-in-law here to worship Devi Katyayani. Just sec how she is worshiping the Devi praying for the wellbeing of your son. Devi was here in her real form. But by seeing you she has accepted this deity form.”

Karala became very pleased to see this personally and blessed all the sakhis. Candravali offered her obeisances to her mother-in-law. Karala blessed Candravali and very affectionately took her back to the house.

After they left. Subala said, “O, Chief of all magicians! Today your knowledge about magic has become successful. ”

Krsna laughed and then proceeded along with Subala towards Radharani's kunja. From afar Radharani could sec Krsna coming towards her kunja. She turned her face so as not to see Krsna. Her face was filled with sadness.

Lalita told Krsna, “You are a cheater. You are always engaged in enjoying with another's wife in a different kunja. Do not proceed here one step more. If you value your wellbeing, then go back to her with whom you were busy enjoying till now. Give up your hope for this place. ”

Hearing from Lalita like this, Krsna spoke in a choked voice, “Lalite! Why are you getting angry with me without any fault? I cannot survive without Radharani for even one moment. She is the controller of My life. Radharani’s name. Her form and qualities are My life and soul. You are my Anuradha. Please save me from this danger. ”

After appealing to Lalita like this, Krsna went towards Radharani with folded hands and sat at Her lotus feet. Radharani’s lotus feet reacted at the touch of His tears. Sri Krsna requested Radharani with a very pathetic voice saying, “Rai! Please save me. Rai! Please save me. ”

Still Radha did not look upon Him even once. Finally, Krsna in sadness and while shedding tears came out of the kunja.

When Visakha saw this, she could not control herself any longer. She said, “Radhe! This is not proper for You to show Your mana \[anger\] to Krsna like this. You know very well that Shri Krsna is naturally wicked. Even knowing this you have developed love for Him. Now You have no other way. But do you know, Radhe, He is very much attached to you. He cannot stay even for one moment without You. That is why You must please follow my request. Now give up Your anger and try to please Your lover. ”

When Radharani heard this from Visakha she became very angry and replied, “Visakhe! What kind of my most dear and chief sakhi are you? You are also requesting me again and again about this wicked Krsna? ”

Visakha then informed Krsna about Radha's severe anger towards Him. He said, “If Radharani will not be happy with Me then I will give up My life. ”

Sakhi Visakha could understand the mental pain and suffering of Sri Krsna and said. "Nandanandana! The only way to please Rai is to meet with Her. ”

Hearing this from Visakha. Krsna very eagerly asked, “Sakhi! Please tell me how can I meet Radharani? "

Visakha replied. "You can come there in the form of Syamasakhi. holding Vina in Your hand. We will glorify You and instruct You to play on the vina. When you play on the vina. Rai will definitely be pleased with you. ”

Inspired by Visakha s idea, Krsna dressed Himself like a musician and holding a vina in His hand Krsna went to the assembly of Radharani and all the sakhis.

The sakhis allowed the musician to sit down. Radharani asked, “Who is this person? "

Visakha replied, “She is Syamasakhi. Her service is to play on the vina. She has come here after hearing of Your great qualities. "

Then Sri Radha ordered Syamasakhi to play on her instrument. Krsna, who is the guru of all musicians, started to play her vina, creating a sweet sound vibration. When Radharani heard the nectarean sound of the vina. She was in great ecstasy. Immediately She embraced Krsna, who was disguised in the form of Syamasakhi. Syamasakhi also embraced Radharani and kissed Her lips. By seeing this, all the sakhis exclaimed, “Jaya! Java! "

In this way Radharani’s anger was removed. The place where this lila was enacted is in Varsana and is called 'Managarh.'

## Appendix

## Krsna cannot be achieved without the mercy of Radharani

Krsna Himself has said:

*“vina radha-prasadena, mama krpa na labhyate"*

**Without the mercy of Radharani no one can get My mercy.**

“Once Lord Siva asked Lord Krsna, “How can one attain You? ” Sri Krsna replied, “A person who surrenders to Me, but not to My beloved Radha never attains Me. A person who once surrenders to Her, and tells Her, 'I am Yours, ’ attains Me also without doing anything further. A person who desires to conquer Me should surrender with all his heart to My beloved Radhika. In this way, one becomes dear to Me. You should take shelter of My beloved Radha, chant My names, and always stay in My abode, Sri Vrndavana. ” (Sanat-Kumara-Samhita)

## Glories of the holy name of Radharani

*'ra' sabadoccaranadeva sphito bhavati madhavah*  
*'dha' sabdoccaratah pascaddhavatyeva sasambhramah.*

**Merely by the utterance of the syllable ‘ra’ Krsna feels ecstasy and by the utterance of the syllabic dha’ Krsna follows behind the pronouncer very respectfully. (Brahma Vaivarta Purana 4.52.36)**

Sripada Prabodhananda Sarasvati:

‘Those who do not worship Radharani, even if they achieve Syama sindhu- Ocean of Syama, in reality they get only a bindu - one drop of Syama. Not more than that. '’

## Meditation on Radharani

The following meditation on Srimati Radharani comes from the Sanat- Kumara-Samhita:

“I meditate on Sri Radhika standing next to Krsna. Radha wears a fine blue dress, and Her beautiful bodily complexion resembles molten gold. Radharani half-covers Her beautiful smiling lotus face with the border of Her garment. Radha’s restless eyes dance like cakori birds on Her charming face. With Her thumb and forefinger, Radha offers tambula to the lotus mouth of Her beloved. pearl necklace adorns Radha's lovely raised breasts.

“Radhika’s waist is slender, and Her wide hips are ornamented with a kinkini-mala \[a necklace of tiny tinkling golden bells\]. Radharani wears jeweled earrings, armlets, and bracelets. Golden ankle bells jingle sweetly at Radha’s lotus feet, and Jeweled rings crown Her toes. Sri Radha is very graceful.

"All the parts of Her transcendental body are indescribably beautiful. Radhika is very happy, satisfied, and in the prime of youth. Sri Radha is the very form of love for Krsna, and She always stands beside Him completely drowned in ananda rasa. "

## Two kinds of manifestations of Srimati Radharani

Just like Nanda-Nandan Krsna never leaves Vrndavana. Same way Vrsabhanu nandini Radharani also never leaves Vrindavana. But we have heard that Radharani went to Kuruksetra to meet with Sri Krsna and also Radharani entered Yamuna and went to Surya loka. Later She was transferred to New Vrindavana which is situated in Dwaraka. In reality Radharani Her self did not go. She never leaves Vrindavana like Sri Krsna. Radharani has two expansion forms. One is Sanyogini Radha and another is Viyogini Radha.

Sanyogini Radha also known as “Kama"’ Radha. In this form Radharani had entered in to Yamuna river and was transferred to Surya loka. Later she was brought to New Vrindavana in Dwaraka.

Viyogini Radha also known as “Varna” Radha. In this form Radharani went to Kuruksetra to meet with Krsna.

Vrisabhanu Nandini Radharani knows it very well that her husband never leaves Vrindavan. When Sri Krsna goes to Mathura in His Vasudeva form at that time Nanda-Nandan Krsna disappears in Vrindavana. As a result of this original Radharani enjoy “pravasakhya” rasa.

## Songs by Srila Bhaktivinoda Thakura

## Radha-bhajane jadi

Translation By Kusakratha Prabhu

*radha-bhajane jadi mati nahi bhela*  
*krsna-bhajana tava akarana gela*

If your desire for the worship of Srimati Radharani does not come about, then your so-called worship of Krsna is completely useless.

*atapa-rohita suraya nahi jani*  
*radha-virahita madhava nahi mani*

Just as I never know the sun to be without sunlight, so I do not care to regard Madhava without Radha.

*kevala madhava pujaye so ajnani*  
*radha anadara koro-i abhimani*

One who worships Madhava alone is imperfect in his knowledge, and one who disrespects Radha is simply conceited and proud.

*kabani nahi korobi tankor sanga*  
*citte icchasi jadi braja-rasa-ranga*

You should never associate with such a person if you at all desire within your heart to participate in the eternal sportive pastimes of Vraja.

*radhika-dasi jadi hoy abhiman*  
*sighra-i mila-i taba gokula-kan*

If one considers oneself to be a humble maidscrvant of Radhika, then such a person very quickly meets the Lord of Gokula.

*brahma, siva, narada, sruti, narayani*  
*radhika-pada-raja pujaye mani'*

Lord Brahma, Lord Siva, Narada Muni, the personified Vedas, and Laksmidevi all honor and worship the dust of Radhika's lotus feet.

*uma, rama, satya, saci, candrd, rukmini*  
*radha-avatara sabe, -amnaya-vani*

The Vedic scriptures declare that the goddesses Uma. Rama. Satya, Saci, Candra, and Rukmini are all personal expansions of Srimati Radharani.

*heno radha-paricarya jankara dhan*  
*bhakativinoda ta‘r magaye caran*

Bhaktivinoda, whose only treasure is the scrvice of such a Radha, humbly begs for Her lotus feet.

## Radhika-carana-padma

Translation by Kusakratha Prabhu

*radhika-carana-padma, sakala sreyera sadma,*  
*jatane je nahi aradhilo*  
*radha-padmankita dhama, brndabana jar nama,*  
*taha je na asroy korilo*

He who has failed to carefully worship the lotus feel of Srimati Radhika, which are the abode of all auspiciousness; he who has not taken shelter in the transcendental abode known as Vrndavana, which is decorated with the beautiful lotus flower named Radha...

*radhika-bhava-gambhir, citta jeba mahadhir,*  
*gana-sanga na koilo jivane*  
*kemone se syamananda, rasa-sindhu-snanananda,*  
*labhibe bujhoha eka-mane*

... he who in this life has not associated with the devotees of Radhika, who are very wise and whose devotion for Radha is very deep-how will such a person ever experience the bliss of bathing in the ocean of Lord Syama’s sublime mellows? Please understand this most attentively.

*radhika ujjvala-raser acarya*  
*radha-madhava-suddha-prem vicarya*

Srimati Radhika is the exemplary teacher of the brilliant mellows of conjugal love. This pure love between Radha and Madhava is worthy of discussion and contemplation.

*je dharilo radha-pada parama jatane*  
*se pailo krsna-pada amidya-ratane*

He who has grasped hold of the lotus feel of Radha with great care obtains the lotus feel of Krsna, which are like priceless jewels.

*radha-pada vina kabhu krsna nahi mile*  
*radhar dasir krsna satya-vede bole*

Without taking shelter of the lotus feel of Radha, one can never personally meet Lord Krsna. The Vedic scriptures declare that Krsna is the properly of the maidservants of Sri Radha.

*chodata dhana-jan, kalatra-suta-mita,*  
*chodata karama geyan*  
*radha-pada-pankaja, madhurata sevan,*  
*bhakativinoda paraman*

Abandoning wealth, followers, wife, sons, and friends, and giving up materialistic activities and intellectual knowledge, being absorbed in the sweetness of service to the lotus feet of Srimati Radharani-this is Bhaktivinoda's conviction.

## Bhojana-lalase

Translation by Kusakratha Prabhu

*bhojana-lalase, rasane amar,*  
*sunoha vidhana mor*  
*sri-nama-yugala-raga-sudha-rasa,*  
*khaiya thakoha bhor*

O my longue, you who are overwhelmed with the desire for tasting material enjoyment, please hear my instructions. Always remain deeply absorbed in drinking the nectarean loving mellows of the beautiful names of the divine couple Radha and Krsna.

*nava-sundar piyusa radhika-nam*  
*ati-mista manohar tarpana-dham*

The name of Radhika is ever-fresh and lovely, and is pure ambrosia. It is very sweet, utterly enchanting and is the abode of complete satisfaction.

*krsna-nam madhuradbhuta gadha dugdhe*  
*ativa jatane kr misrita lubdhe*

With great care, you should eagerly blend this ambrosial name of Radhika with the wonderful sweet condensed milk of the name of Krsna.

*surabhi raga hima ramya tanhi ani'*  
*aharaha pan koroha sukha jani'*

Now add into that mixture the sweet fragrance of loving affection, which is both cool and delightful. Drink this nectar day and night, and you will know what true happiness is.

*nahi rabe rasane prakrta pipasa*  
*adbhuta rasa tuwa puraobo asa*

No longer will there remain on the longue a thirst for mundane tastes, for these wonderful transcendental mellows will fulfill all of your desires.

*dasa-raghundtha-pade bhaktivinod*  
*jaco-i radha-krsna-nama pramod*

At the lotus feel of Raghunatha dasa Goswami, Bhaktivinoda begs for ecstatic rapture in hearing and chanting the divine names of Radha and Krsna.

## Song by Srila Gaurakisora Dasa Babaji

Gaurakisora Dasa Babaji spent thirty years wandering through Vraja Feeling separation from the Divine Couple, he would loudly chant the holy names in a deep voice full of lamentation. An excerpt from one of his favorite bhajanas is included below:

*kothay go premamayi / radhe radhe!*  
*radhe radhe go jaya / radhe radhe!*  
*dekha diye prana rakha / radhe radhe!*  
*tomara kangala tomaya dake / radhe radhe!*

*radhe vrnddvana-vilasini / radhe radhe!*  
*radhe kanu-mana-mohini / radhe radhe!*  
*radhe asta-sakhira siromani / radhe radhe!*  
*radhe vrsabhanu nemdini / radhe radhe!*

"O Radhe! Radhe! Where are You? goddess of ecstatic love Radhe! Radhe!All glories to You. Radhe! Radhe!  
“O Radhe! Radhe! Please reveal Yourself to me and thereby maintain my life. This most despicable fallen servant of Yours calls out to You. Radhe! Radhe!  
‘'O Radhe! Oh artful enjoyer of Vrndavana. Radhe! Radhe! Oh enchantress of Kanu's \[Krsna's\] mind. Radhe! Radhe!  
“O Radhe! Oh crest jewel of Your eight dearest sakhis. Radhe! Radhe! Oh delightful daughter of Maharaja Vrsabhanu. Radhe! Radhe!

## Prayers

## Radharani Ki Jay! Maharani Ki Jay!

*radharani ki jay, maharani ki jay.*  
*bolo barsanevali ki jay jay jay*  
*thakurani hi jay, hari pyari ki jay*  
*vrsabhanu-dulali hi jay jay*

*gaurangi ki jay, hemangi ki jay*  
*vrajaraja-kumari ki jay jay jay*  
*vrajarani ki jay, vrajadevi ki jay*  
*gahvaravana-vali ki jay jay jay*

All glories to Radharani! All glories to the greatest of all Queens. Sing the glories of Radharani, who lives in Varsana.  
All glories to Radha Thakurani \[Queen Radha\], who is the beloved of Sri Hari. All glories to the beloved daughter of King Vrsabhanu.  
All glories to Radharani who has a fair complexion and golden limbs.  
All glories to the Queen of Vraja, All glories to the goddess of Vraja. All glories to Radha who wanders in the Gahvara forest.

## Sri Radhika Astottara-sata-nama-stotram 108 Names of Radhika

*srimad-radhika rasamayi, rasajna rasika tatha*  
*rasesvari rasa-bhakti, rasa-purna rasa-prada*

Sri Radha is endowed with all the most splendorous opulence, and is completely pervaded with ever-flowing transcendental mellows. She fully understands the subtleties of those mellows. Radha is adept in relishing mellows. Radhika is the Queen of the nightly rasa dance. She is totally devoted to expressing divine mellows, Sri Radha is full to overflowing with pure mellows. She bestows these exquisite mellows unto everyone.

*rangini rasa-lubdha ca, rasa-mandala-karini*  
*rasa-valasini radha, radhika rasa-purnada*

Radha is always very playful. She is greedy to taste newer and newer aspects of mellows. Radha is the designer of the circular arenas for rasa dancing. She enjoys sporting the rasa dance to Her heart's content. Radha is the best worshiper of Syama. Radhika, the best worshiper, is adorned intimately by Her friends. She bestows mellows unto others in the most completely fulfilling way.

*rama-ratna ratna-mayi, ratna-mala su-sobhana*  
*raktosthi rakta-nayani, raktotpala-vidhayini*

Sri Radha is the topmost jewel among all the many forms of Laksmi-devi. All of Her ornaments and paraphernalia are studded with precious jewels. Radhika likes to wear gemstone necklaces. Radha radiates the nicest, purely sublime splendor. Her lips are naturally hued with a charming redness. The corners of Sri Radha's eyes are hued with a charming redness. The corners of Sri Radha's eyes are hued with reddish tints. She especially likes to hold a red lotus flower in Her hand.

*ramani ramini gopi, vrnidavana-vilasini*  
*nana-ranga-victrangi, nana-sukha-mayi sada*

Radharani is a charming young mistress. Her loveliness is especially pleasing to behold. Radha is a cowherd girl. She likes to frolic in the forest of Vrndavana, Radha is fond of wearing different types of gemstones. The shapes of Radharani's limbs are astonishingly lovely to behold. She abounds in various forms of happiness.

*samsara-sara-tarani, venu-gita-vinodini*  
*krsna-priya krsna-mayi, krsna-dhyana-parayani*

Radharani delivers souls out of the material world. She takes great pleasure in listening to flute songs. Radha is very dear to Krsna. She is Lord Krsna's exact counterpart. Radha is fond of meditating upon Sri Krsna.

*krsnanandi ksina-madhya, krsna krsnalaya subha*  
*candravali candra-mukhi, candra ca krsna-vallabha*

Radhika is always full of blissful Krsna consciousness. Her waist is very slim. Radha is the female form of Krsna Himself. She is the refuge of Her beloved Krsna, Radharani is the embodiment of auspiciousness. She is beautiful as a series of full moons. Radha’s face is round, and radiantly soothing like a full moon. She is the very personification of the moon itself. Radha is the dearmost beloved of Lord Krsna.

*vrndavanesvari devi, krsna-rangi para-gatih*  
*dhyanatata dhyana-magna, sada-krsna-kutuhali*

Radharani is the Queen of Vrnda's forest. She is the Supreme Goddess of the entire creation. Radha enjoys playing fun games with Krsna, Srimati Radharani is the final transcendental goal. She is naturally situated far, far beyond anyone's meditation. Radha is very meditative by nature. She is always playing mischievous pranks on Krsna.

*prema-mayi prema-rupa, prema prema-vinodini*  
*krsna-priya sadanandi, gopi-mandala-vasini*

Radhika is effused with the purest ecstatic love. She is the embodiment of love's most beautiful form. Her every thought, action, word and deed is totally saturated with love. She revels in the delightful sports of pure love. Radha is the favorite of Lord Krsna. She is perpetually absorbed in the greatest happiness. Radharani resides within the circle of Her cowherd girlfriends.

*sundarangi ca svarnabha, nila-patta-vidharini*  
*krsnanuragini caiva, krsna-prema-su-laksana*

Radha’s bodily limbs are all very attractive. Her complexion is radiantly golden. Radha especially likes to wear blue silken garments. She is very contented in Her attachment to Krsna, Srimati Radharani is nicely marked by Her obvious love for Krsna.

*nigudha-rasa-sarangi, mrgaksi mrga-locani*  
*asesa-guna-para ca, krsna-pranesvari sama*

Radha embodies variegated forms of confidential mellows. Her eyes are just like those of a dear. Radha views things innocently like a deer. She excels even beyond limitless good qualities. Radharani is the mistress of the vary life-breath of Krsna. She is equal in Her dealings with all.

*rasa-mahamala-madhyastha, krsna-ranga sada sucih*  
*vrajesvari vraja-rupa, vraja-bhumi-sukha-prada*

Radha dances in the center of the circular rasa dance. She enjoys frolicking with Lord Krsna. Radharani always has immaculate manners. Srimati Radharani is the Queen of Vrajadhama. Aspects of Her form are visible in all the natural features throughout Vraja who is the grantor of happiness to all beings in Vraja-bhumi.

*rasollasa-madonmatta, lalita rasa-sundari*  
*sarva-gopi-mayi nitya, nana-sastra-visarada*

Radha especially blossoms with delight during the rasa dance and becomes wildly intoxicated by drinking honey-wine. Radha embodies the most charming loveliness. She is the truly beautiful expression of divine loving mellows. She expands Herself into multiple forms as all the cowherd girls, Radha is the essence of eternity. She is greatly learned in various scriptures.

*kamesvari kama-rupa, sada krsna-parayana*  
*para-sakti-svarupa ca, srsti-sthiti-vinasini*

Radha is the Queen of transcendental lust. She is the personification of divine desire. Sri Radhika is always cherishing Krsna. She is the very figure of divine potency. Radha is the annihilator of the material creation.

*saumya saumya-mayi, radha, radhika sarva-kamada*  
*ganga ca tulasi caiva, yamund ca sarasvati*

Radha is pleasing like the moon. All of Her dealings are soft and gentle. She is endowed with all prosperity and success. Radha fully satisfies Her beloved by Her intimate worship. She fulfills the aspirations of all beings. Sri Radha is the original form of the Ganges River. She is the original form of the holy basil plant. She is the original form of the Yamuna River. Radha is the original form of the Sarasvati River.

*bhogavati bhagavati, bhagavac-citta-rupini*  
*prema-bhakti-sada-sangi premanada-vilasini*

Srimati Radharani is the original form of the Bhogavati river (the Ganges flowing in the Patala region. She is the Supreme female divinity. Radha is the embodiment of Bhagavan s very heart. She is continuously associated with ecstatic loving devotion. Radha cavorts in the ecstatic bliss of pure love.

*sadananda-mayi nitya nana-dharma-parayana*  
*trailokyakarsani adya, sundari krsna-rupini*

Radhika is perpetually permeated with exhilaration. She is the incarnation of agelessness. Radha is devoted to performing various auspicious daily rituals. She is the attractor of the three worlds. Sri Radha is the original source of all that is. She is the absolute manifestation of total beauty. Radharani expresses Herself as the form of Lord Krsna.

*sṁtam-astottaram nama, yah pathet prayatah sucih*  
*pratah-kale ca madhyahne, sandhyayam madhya-ratrike*  
*yatra tatra bhavet tasya krsna-prema-yuto bhavet*

If a pious and clean devotee recites these 108 holy names of Sri Radhika - either in the morning \[6-8: 24 a. m.\], evening \[6-8: 24 p. m.\], or in the middle of the night \[10: 48 p. m. -3. 36 a. m.\] - whenever and however that person lives, they become endowed with ecstatic love for Lord Krsna. ”

## Radha Kripa Kataksa

(by Lord Siva)

*munindra-vrnda-vandite triloka-soka-harini*  
*prasaivia-vaktra-pankaje nikunja-bhu-vilasini*  
*vrajendra-bhanu-nandini vrajendra-sunu-sangate*  
*kada karisyasiha mam krpa-kataksa-bhajanam*

Adored by all the best of sages. Radha removes all the miseries of the three worlds! Her joyful face blooms like a lotus flower! In Vraja secret groves Radha delights in pastimes of love. Radha is the charming daughter of King Vrsabhanu, and She always associates intimately with the son of the King of Vraja. Oh when will You shower upon me Your sidelong glance of over flowing grace?

*asoka-vrksa-vallari-vitana-mandapa-sthite*  
*prabala-bala-pallava-prabharunanghri-komale*  
*varabhaya-spurat-kare prabhuta-sampadalaye*  
*kada karisyasiha mam kripa-kataksa-bhajanam*

Radhika rests upon a flower throne beneath a canopy of creepers and boughs of asoka. Her soft and delicate lotus feet radiate a pinkish glow like coral-colored freshly sprouted leaf buds. Her upraised hand bestows the benediction of fearlessness upon Her devotees. Radha is the abode of abundant opulences. when will She shower upon me Her sidelong glance of over flowing grace?

*ananga-ranga-mangala-prasanga-bhangura-bhruvam*  
*sa-vibhramam sa-sambhramam drg-anta-bana-patanaih*  
*nirantaram vasi-krta-pratiti-nandanandane*  
*kada karisyasiha mam krpa-kataksa-bhajanam*

Radha continuously subdues the Son of Nanda into complete surrender unto Herself by piercing Him with the arrows of loving glances shot from the corners of Her eyes. These glances, laden with amorous delusions, inspire Syama with feelings of the most reverential submission. This is further enhanced by Radha’s crook-edly-bending eyebrows that dance with the most auspicious sportive interest of the God of Love! when will She shower upon me Her sidelong glance of overflowing grace?

*tadit-suvarna-campaka-pradipta-gaura-vigrahe*  
*mukha-prabha-parasta-koti-saradendu-mandale*  
*vicitra-citra-sahcarac-cakora-sava-locane*  
*kada karisyasiha mam krpa-kataksa-bhajanam*

Radhika’s form has a fair complexion brilliantly more effulgent than a combination of lighting, pure gold, and campaka flowers. The splendor of Her face defeats the glory of a million Autumn moons. Radha’s restless eyes move to and fro like young cakora birds, and thus make astonishing expressions from moment to moment. when will She shower upon me Her sidelong glance of overflowing grace?

*madonmadati-yauvane pramoda-mana-mandite*  
*priyanuraga-ranjite kala-vilasa-pandite*  
*ananya-dhanya-kunja-rajya-kama-keli-kovide*  
*kada karisyasiha mam krpa-kataksa-bhajanam*

Srimati Radhika is madly intoxicated with Her own eternal youth. She is adorned with the great delight of Her mood of jealous pouting. Radha is dyed in pure loving affection for Her beloved. She is most expert in all the arts of making love. Srimati is most learned in designing loving sports within the matchless opulent kingdom of glorious forest groves. when will She shower upon me Her sidelong glance of overflowing grace?

*asesa-hava-bhava-dhira-hira-hara-bhusite*  
*prabhuta-satakumbha-kumbha-kumbhi kumbha-sustani*  
*prasasta-manda-hasya-curna-purna-saukya-sagare*  
*kada karisyasiha mam krpa-kataksa-bhajanam*

Srimati Radkhika is decorated with the diamond necklace of feminine charms like amorous gestures, ecstatic moods, and gentle manners. Radha’s breasts are plump like the purest golden water pots or the cranial globes of a young elephant. Radha is like a swelling ocean of happiness full of the soft pollen of Her most glorious mild sweet smile. when will She shower upon me Her sidelong glance of overflowing grace?

*mrnala-vala-vallari-taranga-ranga-dor-late*  
*latagra-lasya-lola-nila-locanavalokane*  
*lalal-lulan-milan-manojna-mugdha-mohanasrite*  
*kada karisyasiha mam krpa-kataksa-bhajanam*

Radhika’s creeper thin arms rhythmically wave and flow like fresh young lotus stems in the ripples of a river. The restless roving glance of Radha’s deep blue eyes dances seductively like vine-lips in the breeze. Her playful and alluring movements entice lovely meetings, and fascinate Mohana Himself to take refuge in Her charms. when will She shower upon me Her sidelong glance of overflowing grace?

*suvarna-malikancita-trirekha-kambu-kanthage*  
*tri-sutra-mangali-guna-tri-ratna-dipti-didhiti*  
*salola-nila-kuntala-prasuna-guccha-gumphite*  
*kada karisyasiha mam krpa-kataksa-bhajanam*

Srimati Radhika wears a pure golden necklace on Her neck, which is as graceful as a lovely conch enhanced with three lines. Radha shines in the splendorous auras of Her three-stranded necklace, woven with small jasmine garlands, and decorated with the three jewels of diamond, emerald and pearl. Her luxuriant black tresses, artfully woven with clusters of flowers, flow in waves towards Her heels. when will She shower upon me Her sidelong glance of overflowing grace?

*nitamba-bimba-lambamana-puspa-mekhala-gune*  
*prasasta-ratna-kinkini-kalapa-madhya-manjule*  
*karindra-sunda-dandikavaroha-saubhagorake*  
*kada karisyasiha mam krpa-kataksa-bhajanam*

Radha's rounded hips are decorated by a belt of dangling flowers. Her charmingly thin waists is decorated by groups of superexcellent tiny tinkling jewel-bells. Srimati Radhika's most beautiful thighs taper gracefully like the trunk of the king of elephants. when will She shower upon me Her sidelong glance of overflowing grace?

*aneka-mantra-nada-manju-nupurarava-skhalat-*  
*samaja-raja-hamsa-vamsa-nikvanati-gaurave*  
*vilola-hema-vallari-vidambi-caru-cankrame*  
*kada karisyasiha mam krpa-kataksa-bhajanam*

Radharani's most captivating anklebells are resounding various Vedic mantras as She walks. They also possess the super excellence of resembling the warbling of a flock of the most noble swans. The elegant movement of Her limbs puts to shame the swaying dance of golden creepers. when will She shower upon me Her sidelong glance of overflowing grace?

*ananta-koti-visnu-loka-namra-padmajarcite*  
*himadrija-pulomaja-virincija-vara-prade*  
*apara-siddhi-rddhi-digdha-sat-padanguli-nakhe*  
*kada karisyasiha mam krpa-kataksa-bhajanam*

Srimati Radhika is humbly bowed to by all the Lord Brahmas in the countless millions of universes created by Lord Visnu. Radha is the bestower of boons upon Parvati \[daughter of the Himalayas\], Saci \[Indra’s wife and daughter of Puloma\] and to Sarasvati \[Brahma's wife\]. Radha’s toenails glow with the radiance of boundless mystic perfections and opulent prosperities. when will She shower upon me Her sidelong glance of overflowing grace?

*makhesvari kriyesvari svadhesvari suresvari*  
*triveda-bharatisvari pramana-sasanesvari*  
*ramesvari ksamesvari pramoda-kananesvari*  
*vrajesvari vrajadhipe sri-radhike namo 'stu te*

Oh Goddess of Vedic sacrifices! Oh Goddess of all pious activities! Oh Goddess of all that is natural and spontaneous! Oh Goddess of all the demigods and goddesses! Oh Goddess of all the knowledge of the three Vadas! Oh Goddess of the enforcement of universal law! Oh Goddess of the most pleasurable forest of Vrndavana! Oh Goddess of the entire realm of Vraja! Oh one and only authority Vraja! Oh Sri Radhika? I offer my most respectful obeisances unto You!

*iti mamadbhutam stavam nisamya bhanu-nandini*  
*karotu santatam janam krpa-kataksa-bhajanam*  
*bhavet tadaiva sancita-trirupa-karma-nasanam*  
*labhet tada vrajendra-sunu-mandala-pravesanam*

May Sri Vrsabhanu-nandini, upon hearing my most astonishing prayer being recited by someone, make that person the constant object of the most merciful side-long glance. Then, as that time, one will feel that all the effects of the three types of karma \[whether entangling one in the past, present or future\] have been completely destroyed. At such a time, one will finally gain entrance into the assembly Nandanandana's eternal loving associates.

## Phala Sruti

*rakayam ca sitastamyam dasamyam ca visuddha-dhih*  
*ekadasyam trayodasyam yah pathet sadhakah su-dhih*  
*yam yam kamayate kamam tam tam prapnoti sadhakah*  
*radha-krpa-kataksena bhaktih syat prema-laksana*

That sadhaka \[practicing devotee\] of purified intelligence, who recites this stava with a fixed mind on the lunar days—full moon, bright astami \[waxing eighth day\], dasami \[tenth day\], ekadasi \[eleventh day\], or trayodasi \[thirteenth day\] - finds that each and every desire that one had will be fulfilled without fail. And by the most merciful side-long glance of Srimati Radhika, one will obtain devotional service which has a very special quality of pure, ecstatic love \[prema\]. All glories to Srimati Radharani

*vama-parsve sthitam tasya radhikam ca smaret tatah*  
*nicina-nila-vasanam druta-hema-sama-prabham*  
*patancalenavritardha- su-smeranana-pankajam*  
*kanta-vaktre nyasta nrityac cakori-cancalekshanam*

I meditate on Sri Radhika standing next to Krsna. Radha wears a fine blue dress, and Her beautiful bodily complexion resembles molten gold. Radharani half-covers Her beautiful smiling lotus face with the border of Her garment. Radha s restless eyes dance like cakori birds on Her charming face.
